# Changelog

All notable changes to the Desk app are recorded here.

The format follows [Keep a Changelog](https://keepachangelog.com/en/1.1.0/), and versions follow [Semantic Versioning](https://semver.org/spec/v2.0.0.html).

## Release 6.11 - The theme sliders tune the office's glass; Sign out is one plain row (`sub/glass-sliders`; the Desk app)
The owner, Nov 16: "Yes, do it" (the sliders on the glass, `office-glass.md`'s follow-up), and on the account menu's foot: "This is weird and unnecessary. Just let me sign out". Records: `docs/sub-status/office-glass.md` ("Release 6.11"), `docs/sub-status/account-menu-tidy.md` ("Release 6.11"). Shots `~/ma-coord/design-drafts/office-glass/sliders/`, `~/ma-coord/design-drafts/account-menu/signout/`. Brand review: pass. **Released** as the Desk app `2026.11.16-7f917a4b5` (merge `7f917a4b5`, `release-6-11.md`) to staging and the owner's desk on Nov 16 at 2:41 pm MT.
- **Changed (the glass follows the theme sliders, `web/src/theme/tune/glass.ts`, `apply.ts`, `mirror.ts`, `engine.ts`; `glassCss.ts`, `tileFace.ts`, `tileGlass.ts`, `canvas/sprites.ts`):** Blue moves the glass's cool greys as it moves every other one (the chrome's body, the rim light's, depth's and lift's tints, a tile's grey cast; each app tile's own accent tint stays); Contrast calms or sharpens its light (rim and sheen 0.65x to 1.4x, depth 0.6x to 1.5x, lift 0.75x to 1.25x) and moves the body with the surfaces, as far as every ink's floor allows. The DOM glass follows a slider live; the canvas's tiles raster once when it rests, never per input event. This browser's copy carries the glass, so the next load paints it with its first frame. At the default nothing changes (the office is 6.10's pixel for pixel).
- **Changed (the account menu, `web/src/chrome/AccountPanel.tsx`, `AccountMenu.tsx`, `styles/app.css`):** Sign out is one plain row at the menu's foot, drawn as Account settings is; one press signs out, as before. No split button, no chevron, no line under it.
- **Removed (the office's menu):** Sign out everywhere, with its code (`signOut`'s everywhere path, `SignOutEverywhereFailed`, `answeredWithin`, `EVERYWHERE_WAIT_MS`, `sso.ts`'s `everywhereFailed` and its two signed-out lines). It stays in Account settings, the Platform app's Account menu. A member signed out everywhere from elsewhere still ends here as any dead session does.
- **Changed (first load):** 248.1 KB gz (6.10: 248.9): the glass's lens (the frosted, bent wood under a tile, idle time only) is a chunk of its own, `canvas/glassLens.ts`.
- **Accessibility:** text 4.5:1 and icons 3:1 on the glass at every slider position (unit at the corners, DOM and tiles; 360 words and icons rendered across 15 shots, none under). Reduced transparency and more contrast are 6.10's.
- **Fixed (tests):** `windowlift.shell.spec.ts` expected the open tile's 6.9 frame; since 6.10 it wears the glass by design. The spec checks the glass frame, and the old one under reduced transparency.
- **Tests:** vitest `tune/glass.test.ts` (new), `tune/apply.test.ts`; e2e `themeSliders.glass.shell.spec.ts` (new), `signOut.shell.spec.ts` (was `signOutEverywhere.shell.spec.ts`), `accountMenu.shell.spec.ts`, `sso.shell.spec.ts`, `signedInCookie.shell.spec.ts`, `release.spec.ts`; shots `themeSliders.glass.shots.shell.spec.ts`, `accountMenu.shots.shell.spec.ts` (`foot-*`).

## Release 6.10 - The office in glass, the account button and menu with it (`sub/office-glass`; the Desk app)
The owner, Nov 16, after the app tiles' glass C: "Maia's circle needs the same effect … Probably all of the UI elements in office actually." Then, after the sheet: "Ship it" (its two calls as built: menus, popovers and notices opaque glass, the bars' body 96 to 98 %, widgets' cards in the office blue). Record: `docs/sub-status/office-glass.md`; sheet `~/ma-coord/design-drafts/office-glass/pick-sheet.png`; the account menu's shots `~/ma-coord/design-drafts/office-glass/account-6.10/` and `shipped/`. Merged to ma-desk main `5488fddba`. **Released** as the Desk app `2026.11.16-5488fddba` to staging (11:11 am MT) and the owner's desk (11:11 am MT) on Nov 16 (`docs/sub-status/release-6-10.md`).
- **Changed (the office's chrome, `web/src/styles/glass.css`, `app.css` and each part's own stylesheet):** the top bar's round buttons, scope track, chips and held line; the bottom bar's hints, desk switch, zoom and edit row; the bandwidth and setup circles and the bandwidth panel; every notice in the shell's stack; a desk's menu, the hidden list, the scope list, the bell's panel, the mark's menu and each app's zoom popout; the open tile's frame; the Agent app's mark: glass C, with the light as their edge in place of the hairline. The bars wear a dense body (text 4.5:1 over anything behind), and menus, popovers and notices an opaque one, since with no frost in CSS words under them would read as a ghost of what's behind.
- **Changed (the canvas):** widgets' cards are glass, in the office blue's egg, their words at 4.5:1 on every desk style; a tile in hand shows its desk frosted under it, from one copy made in idle time after the press.
- **Changed (the account button and menu, `web/src/chrome/AccountMenu.tsx`, `AccountPanel.tsx`, `app.css`):** the account menu is opaque glass like the other menus: it scrolls, so a plate (`.account-glass`) wears the glass and holds it, and the member's pinned header draws the plate's own ground, so it stays opaque with no seam. The split Sign out keeps its own button ground. The account button has its glass neighbours' edge (a clear ring with the rim light, their depth and lift); a hover, a press or the open menu still strengthens the ring.
- **Kept:** windows, apps' content, Maia's panel, dialogs and long-text cards.
- **Accessibility:** under reduced transparency or more contrast everything is the last release's look, pixel for pixel, live (release 6.7's for the chrome, 6.9's for the account button and menu). Every word and icon on the glass measured on the rendered surface: text 4.88:1 or more (the account menu's About line in Light; the chrome 4.97), icons 4.93:1 or more. Focus rings unchanged.
- **Fixed (tests, `web/tests/e2e/bandwidth.shell.spec.ts`):** ten bandwidth specs failed in the first half-day of a UTC week ("Resets Nov 22 5:00 PM" where they expected a weekday). The test was wrong, not the words: its week ran from Monday 00:00 UTC rather than the member's own zone (TD-226), and it expected a weekday however far off the reset was, where the words rule (`formatReset`) says a date beyond 6.5 days. The fixture now uses the browser's zone and the expectations follow the rule; the words are unchanged.
- **Tests:** vitest `glassCss.test.ts`, `tileGlass.test.ts`, `glassKeys.test.ts`; e2e `officeglass.shell.spec.ts`, and `hidden.shell.spec.ts`, `markMenu.shell.spec.ts`, `opentiles.shell.spec.ts` for the scrolling list, the mark menu's surface and the open tile's frame; `accountMenu.shell.spec.ts` reads the split button's edge against the plate's glass; contrast `officeglass.contrast.shell.spec.ts` (with the account menu and each section open); shots `officeglass.shots.shell.spec.ts` (`account`, `accountopen`, `accountphone`).

## Release 6.9 - The account menu's polish: the header pinned, Sign out a split button (`sub/account-menu-polish`; the Desk app)
Release 6.8's brand review left two calls, which the coordinator made (Nov 16). Record `docs/sub-status/account-menu-tidy.md` ("Polish"); before and after stills `~/ma-coord/design-drafts/account-menu/polish/`. Merged to ma-desk main `a02d606e5`. **Released** in the Desk app `2026.11.16-6445d774f` with the two others in release 6.9 (`docs/sub-status/release-6-9.md`), staging and the owner's desk.
- **Fixed (`web/src/chrome/AccountPanel.tsx`, `styles/app.css`):** opening a section that made the menu scroll cut the member's header mid-line (640x360 with Appearance open, a phone with Maia open). The header (avatar, name, tier) now stays pinned at the menu's top in the menu's own colour, with a hairline under it only while something has scrolled under it, and a section's scroll lands on whole lines, so nothing at the top is cut mid-line. The scroll goes only as far as the open section needs, follows what loads into it as it opens, glides, and is instant under reduced motion. The keyboard's focus is never brought into view under the header. On a short, wide screen each column stays put on its own, so only the column with the open section scrolls (the other no longer scrolls away with it).
- **Changed:** Sign out is a button of its own (the office's button look: its edge, ground and weight), which signs out at once as before, with no confirm. Beside it, past a divider, "More ways to sign out" is a separate control (a split button, 36 px, 44 with a finger), whose chevron opens Sign out everywhere under it (`aria-expanded`). Words unchanged.
- **Tests:** `accountMenu.shell.spec.ts` (with each section open at 1440x900, 1024x768, 390x844 and 640x360 in three themes: the header whole and uncovered, nothing cut at the top, no scroll when it fits; the split button's parts, divider, edge contrast and keyboard; axe on it shut and open at every size; reduced motion); `accountMenu.shots.shell.spec.ts` (settled shots, Sign out's focus ring).

## Release 6.9 - The built avatar's backgrounds are all cool: avatar kit 1.1.0 (`sub/avatar-kit-cool`; the Desk app and Commons, with ma-platform)
The owner's standing rule, "no green, brown or warm tints in any MA UI": the avatar kit's background swatches still offered green, amber, rose, pine and ochre. Record `docs/sub-status/avatar-kit-cool.md`; before/after sheet `~/ma-coord/design-drafts/avatar-kit-cool/`. **Released** in the Desk app `2026.11.16-6445d774f` with the two others in release 6.9 (`docs/sub-status/release-6-9.md`), staging and the owner's desk.
- **Changed (`packages/avatar-kit`, the Designer's kit 1.1.0, copied over whole; the same files as ma-platform's):** slots 1, 2, 3, 7 and 8 are mist, sky, periwinkle, blue-grey and denim (were green, amber, rose, pine and ochre); blue, violet, slate, steel and deep-slate are as they were. A recipe stores the slot's index, so a member's avatar keeps its slot and draws the new color; nothing to migrate. The kit's own tests cover the backgrounds (both coolness tests, the distances, the art's floors, every slot's stored recipe, the face rule on every swatch and skin tone).
- **Tests:** `avatarRefresh.shell.spec.ts` looks for slot 3's new color (`#C7C9E6`, periwinkle).

## Release 6.9 - The member avatar's initials tints are all cool (`sub/avatar-cool-tints`; the Desk app, with ma-platform)
The owner's standing rule, "no green, brown or warm tints in any MA UI": Light and Dark still drew the badge spec's green, amber and rose initials tints. Record `docs/sub-status/avatar-cool-tints.md`; before/after sheet `~/ma-coord/design-drafts/avatar-tints/`. **Released** in the Desk app `2026.11.16-6445d774f` with the two others in release 6.9 (`docs/sub-status/release-6-9.md`), staging and the owner's desk.
- **Changed (`web/src/theme/tokens.ts` `avatar.*`, `web/src/chrome/avatar.ts`, the avatar block of `styles/app.css`):** the six tints are blue, denim, mist, periwinkle, steel and slate, in the slots of blue, green, amber, rose, violet and slate, so every member keeps their slot (`hueOf` unchanged); the ids were renamed with their colours, and nothing stores one. Zen's set, already cool, is unchanged; Light and Dark take it, told apart by lightness as much as hue. Every ink 4.5:1 or better on both stops. The Platform and Guild apps hold the same values (ma-platform `account.css` `--k-av-*`), shipped there first.
- **Added (`docs/assets/brand/ui/tokens.json`):** the kit carries `avatar.*` in every theme, with `renamedFrom` and `was`, and an `avatar` note.
- **Tests:** `tokens.test.ts` (each ink on both stops, every stop cool, the six at least 0.045 apart in OKLab), `avatar.test.ts` (the renamed slots).

## Release 6.8 - The account menu, tidied: never over 60 % of the page, configs one level down (`sub/account-menu-tidy`; the Desk app)
The owner, Nov 16: "the Avatar menu needs to be cleaned up... Vertically, that menu should never extend more than 60% of the page. Configs and options should be tucked in expansions or modals." Record `docs/sub-status/account-menu-tidy.md`; before and after stills `~/ma-coord/design-drafts/account-menu/before/` and `after/`. **Released** with the avatar below as the Desk app `2026.11.16-53dbee1bc` (ma-desk `53dbee1bc`) to staging and the owner's desk on Nov 16 at 4:55 am MT (staging) and 5:04 am MT (his desk) (`docs/sub-status/release-6-8.md`). Its full brand review lined Background's swatches up with the Appearance section's labels (`sub/brand-account-menu-tidy`).
- **Changed (`web/src/chrome/AccountPanel.tsx`, `styles/app.css`):** the menu is never taller than 60 % of the page, at any size, and its first view fits whole: the member, Theme (its label beside the radios), a row for Appearance and one for Maia, Keyboard shortcuts, About this desk (the owner), Account settings, Report a safety concern, Sign out. Rows are 36 px with a mouse (44 with a finger). On a short, wide screen (a phone on its side) it's two columns.
- **Changed (one level down, one at a time):** Appearance holds "Use Zen when the system is dark", Blue and Contrast with their Reset, Background and "Show setup and what's new"; Maia holds her switch and rows, with "On" or "Off" on its row; Sign out everywhere is behind the chevron beside Sign out ("More ways to sign out"). Words unchanged, but for the new rows' names.
- **Changed (`web/src/chrome/ShortcutsDialog.tsx`):** Keyboard shortcuts is a modal over the menu, the install offer at its foot. Esc closes it and only it, back to its row; a second Esc closes the menu.
- **Tests:** `accountMenu.shell.spec.ts` (60 % at 1440x900, 1024x768, 390x844 and 640x360 in three themes, a member and the owner with everything; each section; the modal's keyboard; axe), `accountMenu.shots.shell.spec.ts`; the specs that reach a tucked part open it first (`openMenuSection`).

## Release 6.8 - The account button is its avatar; a new avatar shows without a reload (`sub/account-menu-avatar`; the Desk app)
The owner, Nov 16, on the top bar after making a custom avatar: "the thing is awkward", and "I had to refresh the page to see my avatar". Record `docs/sub-status/account-menu-avatar.md`; shots `~/ma-coord/design-drafts/account-menu/avatar/`. **Released** in the Desk app `2026.11.16-53dbee1bc` with the menu above (`docs/sub-status/release-6-8.md`).
- **Changed (`web/src/chrome/Avatar.tsx`, the avatar block of `styles/app.css`):** the account button is the avatar, the same 38 px round as the shield, full screen and bell (36 in short-viewport mode) with their 1 px edge, which a hover, a press or the open menu strengthens; no grey ring round a smaller circle. A built avatar is framed 1.14 times closer, head centred, room above the hair, shoulders still in, and the button and the menu take the kit's full artwork (one image). The tier's seal is lighter: 12 px on the button and 13 on the menu's 40 (was 12 on 32 and 14).
- **Fixed:** with a picture or built avatar the account button sat 6 px below the bar's other buttons and the top bar was 12 px taller (the button hung on its image's baseline); it's level now.
- **Fixed:** `/v1/me` is read again whenever Account settings closes and on coming back to the tab (at most once a minute), so an avatar or name changed there or in another tab shows with no reload; the old avatar stays drawn until the new one's image is decoded, in the button and the menu's header.
- **Tests:** vitest `avatarSwap.test.ts`, `meReread.test.ts`; e2e `avatarRefresh.shell.spec.ts`, `avatar.shell.spec.ts` (the button's size and edge at 38 and 36 px); shots `AVATAR_SHOTS=<dir>` (`avatar.shots.shell.spec.ts`).

## Release 6.7 - App tiles in glass, C; the office's one CSS glass on the mode chooser and Maia's bubble; "Still loading" only while it is (`sub/app-tiles-glass-c`; the Desk app)
The owner, Nov 16, on 6.6's bevel: "a bit tacky… I want a macOS liquid look". Of the three glass looks on the sheet (`~/ma-coord/design-drafts/app-tiles/glass/`), his pick: "I want C restrained for the app tiles". Then, for the same release: "the same effect… over the mode picker… The active option gets the effect", and "Maia's circle needs the same effect… Probably all of the UI elements in office actually." Record `docs/sub-status/app-tiles-glass.md`; ADR 0064's Nov 16 amendment. **Released** as the Desk app `2026.11.16-b129a9dab` (ma-desk `b129a9dab`) to staging and the owner's desk on Nov 16 at 3:30 am MT (`docs/sub-status/release-6-7.md`).
- **Changed (every icon's tile, agents' included; `web/src/canvas/glass.ts`, `sprites.ts`, `GLASS_FACE` in `tokens.ts`, `web/src/theme/tileGlass.ts`):** glass, restrained, on by default. The tile's tint is the glass, mostly solid, with a textured desk a little through it, frosted and bent at the rim. A thin light hugs the rim, brightest at the top left and fading to a faint glint at the bottom right. Depth comes from light, and the lift is a contact shadow under a soft one. The bevel and the shaded foot are gone. Every icon's ink stays 3:1 or more on its tile over every desk style: the body rises to solid where a desk would wash one out (Light on Walnut). Cached bitmaps only: the desk under the glass is made in idle time after a settle, never in a frame. The prototype's looks A and B and its `localStorage` switch are gone.
- **Accessibility:** under reduced transparency or more contrast, a tile is 6.6's solid tile exactly, live, with no glass work. Widgets' cards and the open tile keep 6.6's face for now.
- **Added (`web/src/styles/glass.css`, `--ma-glass-*` from `web/src/theme/glassCss.ts`):** one CSS recipe for glass on any element of the office: the class `ma-glass`, or a selector in its lists. It uses static gradients and no `backdrop-filter`. Text stays 4.5:1 and icons 3:1 on it, and each wearer falls back to its own look under reduced transparency or more contrast.
- **Changed (the mode chooser, `web/src/maiamode/chooser.css`):** the highlighted option's card is glass in place of the bevel, foot shade and glow. It still moves with the focus, and the other option is unchanged.
- **Changed (Maia's bubble, `web/src/maia/maia.css`):** her circle at the bottom right is glass, and she's drawn over its light. CSS only: her art and the widget are unchanged.
- **Fixed (the chooser, `web/src/maiamode/stillLoading.ts`, `onOfficeReady` in `chrome/loadingOverlay.ts`):** "Still loading your office…" showed 10 s after any chooser appeared, even over an office that had loaded. It now shows only if the office still hasn't said it's ready by then, and fades away once it does (at once under reduced motion).
- **Tests:** vitest `tileGlass.test.ts`, `glassKeys.test.ts`, `glassCss.test.ts`, `stillLoading.test.ts`, `loadingOverlay.test.ts`; e2e `apptiles.glass.shell.spec.ts`, `maiaMode.shell.spec.ts`; shots `apptiles.glass.shots.shell.spec.ts`, perf `apptiles.glass.perf.shell.spec.ts`.

## Release 6.6 - App tiles: an accent tint, a clean bevel and lift, a very subtle egg; no glow; Courier coral (`sub/app-tiles`; the Desk app)
The owner, Nov 15 night, on 6.5's agent tiles: "Whatever inner glow effect you put on the agents really sucks. I don't like it. I want proper, clean bevel and lift. I suspect that the existing bevel effect can just be enlarged." With his APP-TILES and COURIER-ACCENT rows. Record `docs/sub-status/app-tiles.md`; sheet and shots `~/ma-coord/design-drafts/app-tiles/`. **Released** as the Desk app `2026.11.16-4fa50a60c` (ma-desk `4fa50a60c`, the toned-down second cut) to staging on Nov 15 at 11:21 pm MT; the owner's desk waits for idle (`docs/sub-status/release-6-6.md`).
- **Removed:** the agent tiles' coloured rim wash (6.5). No tile has a rim of colour or an inner glow.
- **Changed (every tile on a desk, `web/src/canvas/sprites.ts`, `web/src/theme/tileFace.ts`, `TILE_FACE` and `TILE_TINT` in `tokens.ts`):** a lift at rest in every theme (Zen's tiles were flat), a crisp bevel lit from the top left (the window lift's lit edge, enlarged to about 1.45 px, with a shaded foot; after the owner's look at the first cut, "tone down the shadowing 1/3 and the bezel effect just slightly", the lift is a third lighter and the bevel about 13 % slighter), and on an icon's tile a calm tint of its app's accent as a very subtle egg-shaped gradient, lightest where the icon sits. Zen and Dark keep the slate cast and give warm accents almost no tint, so nothing reads brown; every accent stays 3:1 or more on its tinted tile. Zen's in-hand shadow is a step deeper. Agents wear the same face in their own colour, on the desk and in the Agent app (`AgentMark`).
- **Changed (Courier's accent, COURIER-ACCENT):** coral, `#C3776C` / `#DD9F94` / `#E9A296` (was clay `#C08572` / `#D9A391` / `#E5AC99`): less brown, still muted, 3:1 or more. The agent colour `clay` follows it, labelled "Coral".
- **Tests:** vitest `tileFace.test.ts`, `look.test.ts`; shots spec `apptiles.shots.shell.spec.ts`.

## Release 6.5 - Agents wear the hat in their own colours; Documents asks the desk's agent; the welcome makes only helpers that can run (`sub/agent-icons`; desk-agent and the Desk app)
The owner, Nov 15 night, on two "snowflake" tiles on his desk (helpers Maia's welcome made, on the default "Team" mark, with no way to run): "we need to make sure that Agent's icons have assignable colors so they use the guy with the hat. There's a primary desk agent right? Because the Documents app has an ask maia feature but it should really be ask desk agent, which should default to the primary agent." Record `docs/sub-status/agent-icons.md`; shots `~/ma-coord/design-drafts/agent-icons/`. **Released** as desk-agent `2026.11.16-75972bf` and the Desk app `2026.11.16-75972bf45` (ma-desk `75972bf45`) to staging and the owner's desk on Nov 15 at 8:16 pm MT (`docs/sub-status/release-6-5.md`); White oak and Maia's notice below rode along.
- **Changed (agents' tiles and marks, `web/agent/src/look/`, `web/src/canvas/glyphs.ts`, `sprites.ts`, `web/src/agent/roster.ts`):** every agent wears the Agent app's own 3D icon, the guy with the hat (the final 3D set's `agent`, drawn by `draw3d.ts`), its hat band in the agent's colour and its tile's rim in a very subtle egg-shaped wash of it (OKLCH, so warm colours keep their hue over Zen's slate; the centre stays the plain tile). The lead keeps the Agent app's own accent. An agent its desk hasn't said a colour for wears it grey, as asleep. `nodes` (the "Team" mark) and `spark` retire to the hat; a library icon or an upload the member picks still shows, drawn in the colour on the same tile. The Agent app's rail, header, cards and window titles show the same.
- **Added (colour):** seven colours, the app accents and the office blue (`sage, amber, rose, blue, harbor, clay, indigo`; `AGENT_COLORS` in `tokens.ts`), each 3:1 or more on the tile in Light, Zen and Dark. A new agent takes the next unused on its desk (desk-agent), sage first: blue is the closest to the lead's indigo. An agent's Look has a Color choice first (seven swatches); the tone wheel is gone from it (the tone is still stored, no longer drawn).
- **Added (desk-agent):** `look.color` on every agent view (`agent.list`, `.get`, `.create`, `.update`, `agent.state`, the proposals' before and after), stored in a new nullable `agents.color` column; `agent.create`/`agent.update {color}` (`bad_request` `color` for an unknown one or the lead's); the lead `indigo`; a create with none takes the next unused (then the least used); at open, the lead and every staff agent without one get theirs, oldest first (the owner's two helpers: sage, then amber). Icons: `hat` replaces `nodes` (default; stored `nodes` and `spark` move to `hat`). The lead's tools: `desk_choices` lists `colors`; `propose_agent` takes an optional `color`, icon and tone no longer required, and the lead is told to leave both out.
- **Changed (Documents):** "Ask Maia" and "Ask Maia about this" are now "Ask {lead}" and "Ask {lead} about this" (the desk's lead by name, "Ask Lead"), with a small arrow for the desk's other agents. The question waits in that agent's composer in its own window, with the document staged as a file (its text; a picture's page 1), never sent by itself; the agent answers in its thread on its own key or the member's credits. The first time, one sheet says so. Search reads "Find a document, or ask Lead". Desk Maia's reader lines ("Maia's note", "What's this?") are hers and unchanged. `web/src/agent/ask.ts`, `apps/documents/AskAgent.tsx`; the Agent app's `handoff` and the composer's `initialFiles`.
- **Fixed (the welcome, `web/src/welcome/apply.ts`; desk-agent `agent.create {ifRunnable}`):** a helper is made only if it can run now (the member's MA credits usable, or a key given); otherwise the desk makes nothing (`precondition_failed` `cant_run`, and a create whose credits bind fails after is taken back), the item leaves the reveal list and one line says "I didn’t add helpers yet: they need credits or your own AI key, which you can add in Agent."
- **Tests:** vitest `look.test.ts` (colours 3:1 on the tile, the rim keeps its hue, the order), `ask.test.ts`, `proposalLook.test.ts`, `tileSync.test.ts`; desk-agent `LookIdsTest` (colours equal the app's), `AgentsTest`, `ProposalsTest`, `TeamSchemaTest`, `AgentServiceCreditsTest`; e2e `agent.shell.spec.ts` (hat tiles and the colour choice in L/Z/D), `documents.shell.spec.ts` "Ask Lead", `welcome.shell.spec.ts` with and without credits.
## Unreleased - White oak, a sixth desk style (`sub/white-oak`; the Desk app; in no release)
The owner, Nov 15: "I want a white oak, not quite as white as Plain but close and wood textured like oak with slight strands of very light and desat tan", then on the first draft, "Grain is fine. Needs to be closer to white." Design and every figure: `~/ma-coord/design-drafts/desk-textures/white-oak/` (`README.md`, `pick-sheet-v2.png`).
- **Added:** "White oak" (`white-oak`) in a desk's Desk style row, after Light oak: the shipped oak grain graded near-white (Light `#FBF9F6`, L* 98; Plain is `#FDFDFD`) with faint, desaturated tan only in its darker strands; Zen `#606162`, a cool grey with a hint of tan in the grain; Dark `#4B4B4A`, a pale-oak graphite, the lightest wood there. Three 1024 tiles at density 2, as every style (103 to 115 KB). Light oak stays the default.
- **Light etch:** a near-white desk can't hold the Light rule's light letters, so White oak takes Plain's cut, tinted from the wood: fill `#9D9C9B` 2.61:1 under the desk, wall `#555454` (2.75:1 under the fill), white lip. Desk text is `text.secondary` in Light and Zen and `#BEC2C9` in Dark, 4.82 to 5.9:1, and at least 4.55:1 on the tile's lightest or darkest 1 %.
- **Changed:** the desk menu is 236 px wide (was 220) and its swatches 8 px apart (were 12), so six fit on one row.
- **Zen's no-warm rule (ADR 0093, White oak addendum):** White oak's Zen inks have no warm hue (`deskStyles.test.ts`, unchanged). Its tile is the one exemption, for its grain only: `zenWood.shell.spec.ts` checks the mean isn't warm, at most 12 % of samples are, none past S 0.15, and three in four of those are darker than the mean. The other three Zen woods stay at zero warm pixels.
- **Tests:** `deskStyles.test.ts` (the order, every style's floors), `deskStyle.shell.spec.ts` and `release.spec.ts` (the six names; White oak's other themes aren't fetched), `zenWood.shell.spec.ts` (the exemption).

- **Changed:** the notice's "What goes to an AI provider" says desk agents run on the member's own AI keys or on Minimalist Alliance's credits (ma-platform ADR 0142, contract `desk-helpers.md`; /privacy carries the same line). Same substance as v2's provider line, so `DISCLOSURE` stays v2 (no re-acceptance).

## Unreleased - The decision tray: papers that need the member's decision (`sub/mm-tray`; desk-agent, deskd and the Desk app; in no release)
The owner, Nov 15: "an assistant putting some brief papers and supporting materials on an executive desk and him needing to sign off or decline or follow up". Design `~/ma-coord/design-drafts/maia-tray/` (SPEC.md); deskd API.md §23 "The tray"; status `docs/sub-status/mm-tray.md`. Ships on with the shield, under its kill switch and "Show everything as it comes". Members never see "paper", "tray" or "Maia Mode".
- **Added (desk-agent):** `maia/Tray` (pure): one paper per pending approval (the shield's merged item split), per report that needs a decision or is blocked, per document in Needs you, per person waiting a day on the member's reply, per to-do due today that came from someone; answers fitted to each by code; a confirm on send, spend, delete and egress with no pick; Later only while the deadline floor allows; "Tell {asker}: not now" after three Laters. `maia/TrayStore` (`maia_tray_papers`, `maia_tray_out`; never free-form text past its sending). `reach.tray` in the room's snapshot, with filed lines. `agent.maia.tray.get`, `.answer` (the member's answer, and the office's word on carrying it), `.later`; audit `maia.tray.*`, ids only.
- **Added (desk-agent, her part):** `paper_note {paperId, line, answers, pick}` (class `own`), only in the batched report run that named that paper, labels through code's filter (a link, an address, an @, a long number or a hidden character drops them all); a REPORT line's optional `ask {question, options, dueAt}` with `needs_decision`/`blocked`, carried as an `ask:` line in the compact report; her note lists the open papers by id and kind only. A report's answer goes back as her brief's follow-up: a code-made `desk_message` under her grant that doesn't wait (role `tray`), so the agent's answer comes back as a report.
- **Added (deskd):** the three methods relayed (`AgentMethods`, Maia input); `docs.byPath {writeup}` finds a write-up's document.
- **Added (the Desk app, `web/src/maiamode/tray.ts`, `shield/Tray.tsx`, `Paper.tsx`, `Materials.tsx`, `MaterialDoc.tsx`, `trayActions.ts`):** the catch-up's Needs you is the stack ("1 of 3", sheet edges, filed lines, the tally); a paper on Maia's tray (the desk's etched source, the time chip, the risk, her line in its inset, materials as soft pills, one primary at most, the box addressed to the asker, Later); the confirm strip (exact words from the payload, the button asleep 600 ms and elsewhere from the first tap); Ask for changes with its note; materials inline (Documents' viewer embedded, the Agent app's card for the payload, Courier's last 5, the to-do) beside the panel, or an 88% sheet with the answers docked on a phone; To-do's Needs you rows and "Go through them"; a card's one thing opens its paper in place; her room's "N need you · Go through them"; `[paper:pp-…]` in her chat draws the home desk's paper. Answers queue on the home desk and this device carries them to the asking desk (approval decide with the payload's hash, todo, docs, Courier), and what waited goes when a desk's channel opens. The observer sends approvals' `risk`/`untrusted` and to-dos' `from`.
- **Added (quick reply, the owner's ask on the tray's question):** a reply-arrow button ("Reply to Rosa") on every shield card, held row and paper about a person's message opens a reply right there, focused, their last message quoted from that desk's Courier at draw time; a drag down (spring settle, transform only, none under reduced motion) or a long press on a phone, and R on a focused card, are shortcuts; Esc closes, Cmd/Ctrl+Enter sends. No second confirm for typed replies: "Sent to Rosa · Undo" for 5 s, then Courier's own send the way the message came (its medium and account); the arrow shows only where that can send. desk-agent: `agent.maia.reach.answer {answer: replied}` (a message's or a document's item: done, a quick act, no Back); `agent.maia.reach.held` items carry `who` for a person's message. Maia never drafts a reply.
- **Security (stand-in read, all fixed and confirmed CLEAR):** Approve shows only once the asking desk's approval loads and its payload hash checks, and that hash goes with the answer (`agent.maia.tray.answer {sha256}`, required for an approve); the office approves only that hash, else "This changed". A queued answer keeps its own reply ids and hash (`maia_tray_out.reply`, `.sha256`), never a rebuilt paper, and a reply, document or to-do answer waits 24 hours at most. The label filter runs on NFKC text and refuses any dotted name (the ideographic full stops too), a slash, a colon and 5 digits across separators; the agent's question heads a paper only when it passes. An approval with no or an unknown class gets the confirm strip ("approve"). Her notes clear on disk after 7 days, and a report's paper still open after 7 days goes.
- **Changed (Documents):** `Viewer`'s `compact` (inside a paper: the page, "Read as text" and "What changed" only, as soft pills).
- **Fixed (from the staging proof):** a card's Review keeps its paper open until it's answered; a move to a Trash says it can be brought back (the Agent app's own words for that tool), never "for good"; filed lines read "Approve", not "Approve…"; the confirm button sleeps from its first frame.
- **Tests:** desk-agent `TrayTest` 14, `TrayFlowTest` 3; deskd `AgentRelayTest`/`MaiaRelayTest` lists; vitest `tray.test.ts` 13; `shield.shell.spec.ts` four tray specs (the stack, confirm, note, Later, phone; quick reply on a card, a paper and a held row, with Undo and the drag).

A due card now holds it (`officeSettled(..., cardDue)`) until the card is shown or its chunk failed. `loadingOverlay.shell.spec.ts` "the list failing: the office's own error card" and "a closed account: its card, over the desks" failed on main by timing (first bad named as `fc771218`, `d464c8d8`, `bf4d98da`, `4a3e4d59` on different rounds); after: the spec 63/63 (3 runs, one worker), the two 16/16; vitest `loadingOverlay.test.ts` adds the due-card cases.

## Unreleased - The focus shield's brand pass: Maia's tray (`sub/mm-shield-brand`; the Desk app; in no release)
The owner, Nov 15, on the shield's screens: "The concept is nice, but it is not on brand. It needs a serious branding pass." Direction `~/ma-coord/design-drafts/maia-shield-brand/DIRECTION.md`; status `docs/sub-status/mm-shield-brand.md`.
- **Changed (the look, `web/src/maiamode/shield/`):** every surface Maia hands something over on (the card, the catch-up, To-do as a sheet, All held, the held line's menu, What reaches you, the resume line) is one lifted material: the tray's top-to-foot gradient with the window lift's lit edge and shadow, sunken insets inside it. Maia's still in her field leads the card, the catch-up, the sheet and settings (none in her room). The source is the desk's etched name (Jost capitals) then who. Pills only: one accent pill per surface, soft pills for time choices, quiet text buttons; an interrupt's why is the accent time chip (no underline), its weight the startup chooser's selection glow in place of the left stripe. The catch-up reads as a short letter: a title and a quieter line, one pill per item with its chips as quiet words, the counting groups folded into one tally, her question in the accent tray, the end line under a short rule. Rows carry the kind's glyph in the Lounge's rounded chip; held items group under their desk's etched name; filters are pill selects; settings sections are sunken groups with the catch-up rhythm and detail as checked lists. On a phone the sheet rises with a grabber; 44 px targets.
- **Added (tokens):** `MAIA_MODE` `trayTop`, `trayFoot`, `trayInset`, `soft`, `softHover`, `pillLit`, `scrim` per theme (`--ma-maia-mode-*`); Zen's lit edge is its near-white.
- **Motion:** the card settles in once, "Not now" folds it into its held line, the sheet slides in from the side (from the bottom on a phone), the catch-up's groups rise 30 ms apart; transform and opacity only, none under reduced motion; the line never moves.

## Unreleased - Documents' second build: Notebook pages and write-ups in the index, one person key for every desk, the shield's "goes with" ids (`sub/mm-documents-2`; deskd, desk-agent and the Desk app; in no release)
What `docs/sub-status/mm-documents.md` left, on its defaults. Status `docs/sub-status/mm-documents-2.md`; deskd API.md §25 ("A document", the methods, "Intake", `desk.docs.writeups`); draft ADR "Documents".
- **Added (deskd):** Notebook's pages in the index (`docs/NotebookDocs`, deskd's own reads of desk-notebook as the owner, every 5 minutes and when Notebook's tree changes; a page follows its changes in place, quiet, in Records unless a rule or to-do says otherwise; a trashed page leaves). Desk Maia's write-ups kept on the desk (`docs/WriteupDocs`, desk-agent's `desk.docs.writeups` by cursor; untrusted, "Made for you by {agent}", quiet; one that held the desk's private data is never read by the reader, and its mark is followed). `NotebookModule.onTreeChanged`, `AgentModule.docsWriteups`.
- **Added (deskd, the person key):** `docs.key.get` (the office's secret back, only once the office set one, with when it was first made) and `docs.key.set {key, at}`; a changed key re-keys every attachment's sender from Courier's own record without reading a byte, and their rules, let-through row, questions and person links follow (`DocsStore.rekey`, `Intake.rekeyCourier`). Replaces re-reading every attachment from the start.
- **Added (the Desk app):** the office gives every one of the member's own open desks the same person key (`apps/documents/personKey.ts`, a chunk started once the page is idle; the earliest made wins, checked again once 20 s after it gave one; never stored in the browser).
- **Changed:** `docs.feed {front: true}` items add `contactIds`, `channelId` (an attachment's Courier ids) and `agentId` (an output's or write-up's agent), and the office passes them in `desks[].docs` (`readDocsFront`, each to its own id shape; a docId must be `d_` and 26 characters), so the shield's "close to what's open" can match a document. docs.db gains `contact_id`, `channel_id`, `agent_id`, `no_reader`, added without a schema bump (a rollback still opens it). Courier's `Queries.documentsAfter` also returns the channel and contact ids.
- **Added (desk-agent):** `desk.docs.writeups` (deskd's own, refused with any caller; `AwayStore.writeUpsAfter`, `writeUpIds`).
- **Fixed (stand-in Security, DOC2-L1, I1 to I4):** a cut Notebook outline still retires a page that left, and only on Notebook's own word; a write-up's readable mark is followed after indexing; a file never stacks onto a page or write-up; a paging cursor that doesn't move ends the read; person links are re-keyed or dropped, never a conflict.
- **Tests:** deskd `DocsSourcesTest` 6 (Notebook, write-ups, the front's ids, the person key and its rules); desk-agent `DocWriteupsSocketTest` 2; vitest `personKey.test.ts` 6, `focusSource.test.ts` (the front's ids). The stand-in desk has a Notebook page, a write-up and the key.

## Release 6.2 gate fixes (`sub/mm-release-6-2-fixes`; the Desk app and a test; Nov 15)
Found by release 6.2's full shell suite. Record `docs/sub-status/release-6-2.md`.

## Unreleased - Desk Maia finishes after she thinks, and files to-dos while away (`sub/mm-away-fixes`; desk-agent and deskd; in no release)
Two bugs the Maia Mode Phase 2 staging proof found (Nov 15). Status `docs/sub-status/mm-away-fixes.md`; API.md §18 "To-do's source kind `desk`", §23 "Her model" and "While the member is away".
- **Fixed (desk-agent, `MaiaServiceProvider`):** about half of her model calls failed at the provider (`unavailable (provider)`) right after a refused or untrusted tool result. Every one followed a call in which the model thought before its tool call (8 of 8 on staging; none of the 7 after a call without thinking failed). Her service moves the note to the newest turn on every call, so each thinking block sent back sat after a changed turn, and Opus 5.5's preserved thinking refuses that with a 400. Her requests now send back only the Responses wire's encrypted reasoning, never `thinking` or `redacted_thinking`.
- **Fixed (deskd `DeskSources`, `DeskMessaging`; desk-agent `MaiaProfile`, `HostedGuard`, `RegistryPolicy`, `AgentTools`, `Away`):** her `todo_add` in runs the member didn't start (a report batch, a check-in) was refused `source_unknown`. deskd now keeps each answer it opens for an agent; her runs name the answers in her thread (`origin: {kind: "maia", answers}`); deskd gives the ones it opened for `maia`, adds `U` (`S` for a private one), so `desk {}` resolves: the `todos` group, done without asking under her To-do switch, the item marked untrusted. Her batch and check-in runs say a to-do from a report names `desk {}`. A label's name from another desk is one plain line (Security's DMA2-L2).
- **Tests:** desk-agent `MaiaThinkingReplayTest` 5 (the staging sequence against a stand-in service that enforces the binding; 4 fail on the old code), `MaiaAwayToDoTest` 3, `MaiaServiceProviderTest` +1; deskd `DeskSourcesTest` +2, `TodoToolsDeskdIntegrationTest` +1. Security: stand-in read `~/work/maia-mode/away-fixes/security.md`, CLEAR; DMA2-L2 fixed, DMA2-L1 left (status doc).

## Unreleased - The focus shield: what reaches the member, and when (`sub/mm-shield`; desk-agent, deskd's relay lists and the Desk app)
The owner, Nov 15: Maia as "a protective focus shield", bringing what's close to the member's work first and holding the rest for a good time, "like a really really good executive assistant"; and yes to the home desk seeing which desk and app the member is on and when they pause (ids only, never leaving their desks). Design `~/ma-coord/design-drafts/maia-shield/CONCEPT.md`; deskd API.md §23 "What reaches the member"; draft ADR `draft-desk-maia.md` decision 13; status `docs/sub-status/mm-shield.md`.
- **Added (desk-agent `maia/Focus`, `Reach`, `Deliver`, `Rhythm`, `Learn`, `Shield`, `ReachItems`, `FocusStore`):** the model of now from the office's `focus` block (ids and times only, in memory), states and breaks; the context-distance score (closeness, urgency by fixed rules, standing, deadline) with the interrupt gate only for code-rule Now and the member's let-through list (identity checked); four levels, budgets, the deadline floor, merging, nothing lost (`agent.maia.reach.held`); catch-ups (9, 1 and 5 by default, at most 7 lines, "That’s all for now."), the morning brief, the return catch-up, the week line; the card's words, riders, Back with the ready-to-resume line, pick-ups; learning from Not now, Not important and Sooner next time (never the gate); `maia_focus_*` rows in `agent.db`. Code decides: no model, no credits. With an office that sends `focus`, the list shows what the shield lets through (no 30-minute batch) and `notice` is the shield's interrupt.
- **Added (evals):** `maia/eval/ShieldScenarioEvalTest`: 27 scenario days (9 personas × 3), four arms, the EA rubric by code; D (the default) holds G1 and G2 on all 27 days, every criterion 3 or more per persona, overall 3.94; A (everything at once) 0.51. Report `target/shield-evals.md`.

- **The focus shield, the office side** (`sub/mm-shield-ui`, deskd API.md §23 "What reaches the member"): Desk Maia's observer now runs in every view while she's on (`web/src/maiamode/focusSource.ts`, one sender shared with the room), with the `focus` block (surface, desk, app and item as ids, people as contact ids, typing and idle as edges, visibility, a per-browser device id, completed actions after 5 s with no input; never a URL, title, tab, key or text; a Files folder hashed in the browser); the held line beside the account menu with Hold everything and Working on, the card from under it (riders, `{who}` and the quote from that desk's Courier at draw time), Back restoring this browser's windows exactly, catch-ups under the line and in the room's To-do column, To-do as a sheet with All held, and **What reaches you** (`shield/Settings.tsx`); `reachFromWelcome` for the welcome's Q4 to Q7; vitest `src/maiamode` 150 (53 new), `shield.shell.spec.ts` 3; the keep-alive is re-armed when an observe changes nothing; `shield.css` kept out of `ma-release.json`'s `css[]`.

## Unreleased - Maia's welcome, direction A "Lounge" (`sub/mm-welcome`; the Desk app; in no release)
The owner approved the build on Nov 15 (design-drafts/maia-welcome/CONCEPT.md, his pick A and his change: at every question the member may type their own reply or tap a choice, whichever they like). Captions and music only at launch (the owner, Nov 15: no voice before launch).
- **Added: the welcome** (`web/src/welcome/`, its own chunk and stylesheet; `web/CLAUDE.md` "Maia's welcome"). A new member's first load (membership active within 24 hours, by the Platform's own rule from `/v1/me`) opens on the threshold over the dimmed office: "Begin with sound", "Begin quietly", "I'll explore myself". Then G1 (the one AI disclosure), G2, and the questions: Q1 to Q3 and Q7 for everyone adult, Q5 and Q8 with Maia's desk role, Q4 and Q6 once the shield ships (its seam, `seams.ts`); teens get Q1 and their own Q2, scripted, with no Platform call. Every question has an input for their own words beside the choices. Each answer gets an acknowledgement from the fixed set and an echo; desk events come in the gaps; the fill steps (names, the list, which email, a breath) only while a desk still builds; "Here's what I heard" with Looks right and Change something; the v2 disclosure retitled as its own sheet with its checkbox; the reveal (the dimming lifts, the callback opens an app, the list of what's set up fills in); "Show me around" or "I'm all set"; "I'll be right here."
- **Added: the interviewer's wire** (`net.ts`, ma-platform contract maia.md "Welcome"): every answer goes to `POST /v1/maia/welcome/turn` and is kept there a day; the model's part (ack, echo, placement of their words, at most two follow-ups, a custom helper, the plan) is used when it answers within 2.5 s and passes the office's own checks, else the scripted echo. Never sent: the member's name, to-dos, names typed under Q4, anything key-like (cleared in the browser, P6), a teen's anything.
- **Added: the apply queue** (`apply.ts`): helpers made with no key (so they take MA's credits where the desk offers them, API.md §24), each with every switch asking first; the member's to-dos; Maia on the home desk with her access and Q5's attention fields; the shield's settings (`agent.maia.reach.set`, behind its seam); "Your setup notes" as a Notebook page; desk names. Through the member's own session, an `idem` per item, one retry, "Didn't take · Try again". Items for a desk still building wait in this browser (`ma.welcome.v1`, a layout mirror key) for a day.
- **Changed:** the tour waits for the welcome and doesn't start by itself after one; Maia Mode's first-load question waits too and is marked answered; the loading overlay has a `welcome` reason and per-holder holds (ADR 0058 amendment); the mark's menu has "Set up my office with Maia" (a redo at Q1).
- **Tests:** `src/welcome/flow.test.ts`, `plan.test.ts`; `tests/e2e/welcome.shell.spec.ts` (the harness's fake interviewer, `welcome` in `POST /__harness`).

## Unreleased - Maia's welcome: the music engine, and "Maia Mode" off every member-facing word (`sub/mm-welcome-words`; the Desk app; in no release)
The owner approved the welcome's build on Nov 15. The welcome UI is built on another branch, which merges this one.
- **Added: the welcome's music engine** (`web/src/welcome/audio/`, README there; CONCEPT.md §7.1 and §7.2). It's our own Web Audio
  engine, with every sound written in code: sine and triangle partials, filtered-noise air, a soft FM glass, and felt-piano-like
  plucks. There are no samples and no licence.
  - **The score:** one key (D major), beatless, phrases of 8 to 12 s with silences of 2 to 6 s.
  - **The layers:** L0 air at the press; L1 pad at +4 s; L2 motif after the first answer, every 20 to 40 s, from 12 variants,
    never one of the last 3; L3 glass on the first desk ready; L4 low on the second. The cadence is 6 to 8 s and ends at the
    time it's given. The hero is one glassy chord (a 2.7 s swell and decay), then silence and the context closes.
  - **The levels:** the music at about -24 LUFS short-term (calculated, not yet metered), a 4 s fade-in, and a brick-wall limiter
    with its ceiling at about -1 dBTP.
  - **The controls:** mute, Music, volume and pause. A hidden tab fades out over 2 s and suspends, then comes back gently.
  - **No voice for launch** (the owner, Nov 15). `duck()` is the seam: -10 dB, 150 ms attack, 800 ms release.
  - **The API:** `createWelcomeAudio` and `WelcomeAudio` in `engine.ts`. About 3.9 KB gzipped, imported only by the welcome's
    lazy chunk.
- **Changed: no member reads "Maia Mode"** (the owner, Nov 15: it's an internal name). The new words, in `maiamode/words.ts`:
  - **The room:** "Maia".
  - **The account switch:** "Maia on my desks".
  - **The way in:** "Switch to Maia" (in the menu and in Support Maia's panel, with "Switch to Maia (Alt+Shift+M)").
  - **The startup choices:** "Maia" and "Desks".
  - **The menu lines:** "Maia leaves your desks as soon as your home desk is awake.", "Delete Maia’s desk history" and
    "Maia’s desk history deleted."
  - **The disclosure:** "Before Maia works on your desks", with "Turn on Maia". Its sections no longer use the term ("With this
    on, she can read…", "Your chats with her on your desks…"). It's still version 2 (`maia-mode-v2`): the wording changed, not
    the substance, so no member accepts again.
  - **The room's problem lines** (`deskSource.ts`): "Maia isn’t on your home desk yet." and its siblings.
  - **Unchanged:** code ids, comments, logs and internal docs.
- **Tests:**
  - `src/welcome/audio/engine.test.ts` (17) and `score.test.ts` (6), with a fake AudioContext.
  - `deskSource.test.ts`, `tests/e2e/maiaMode.shell.spec.ts` and `maiaGlide.shell.spec.ts` (main's, since the merge) assert
    the new words.

## Unreleased - One credits wallet for Desk Maia and the member's agents (`sub/mm-maia-wallet`; desk-agent, deskd tests and the Desk app; ma-platform desk-maia.md 0.4.10 and desk-helpers.md; in no release)
The coordinator's decision, Nov 15: one credits wallet per member for Maia and their agents on MA's credits: the tier's monthly part, the starting credits and top-ups in one balance, spent in that order; day and week amounts pace it; past everything, one lighter share a day per tier.
- **Added (desk-agent):** `provider/ma/CreditsWallet`, one view of the wallet, the newest from Desk Maia's answers or any agent's on MA's credits (`allowance.credits` and `credits`, the same object; `drawingFrom` adds `starting` and `none`), for the room, her note and the Keys list; `limits/CreditDays` counts each agent's `costCredits` a day (`ChatResult.centiCredits`, from the gateway) for the team view's usage per source: Maia's row and every agent on MA's credits carry `spendCredits` and a `source`, never dollars for that usage.
- **Changed (desk-agent):** her note's one wallet line ("Credits: 1,760 left (1,550 of this month's 2,500, 210 starting), shared with the member's agents; …"), pace lines for `starting` and `topUp`, the lighter line with today's share, a plain line for `none`; `credits_used` holds her a minute. Her own day cap is unchanged.
- **Changed (the Desk app, Maia Mode's room):** the foot shows the one wallet ("Credits: 1,760 left · renews December 1", "1,550 of this month’s 2,500 · 210 starting · 300 top-up"), what's in use, the lighter line, and "Your credits are used up for now." for a plan without a lighter share; new lines for `credits_used` and `limit_lighter`. No dollars, no countdowns.
- **Proof:** `docs/sub-status/mm-maia-company.md`, "One wallet (Nov 15)".

## Unreleased - MA Connector for Mac: a member's iMessage and iPhone texts into Courier through MA's own signed Mac app, paired through the sealed relay (`sub/mm-mac-connector`; `connectors/mac`, deskd, courier-envelope, the test kit and the Desk app; ma-platform `sub/courier-devices`, relay.md 0.4.3; in no release)
The owner, Nov 15: "Yes go ahead" to an MA-signed Mac connector, replacing the BlueBubbles setup he found too hard. Draft ADR `draft-courier-mac-connector.md`; status `docs/sub-status/mm-mac-connector.md`; API.md §21 "iMessage through MA Connector for Mac".
- **Added (`connectors/mac`, Swift, SwiftPM, macOS 14+, Apple silicon and Intel):** the menu-bar app MA Connector. A code to type into Courier (no sign-in, no password, no port or tunnel on the Mac); a "Connect this Mac?" step naming whose office used the code (masked email) and the pairing's check number, before anything flows; guided Full Disk Access and Messages permission screens with "Check again"; Messages' database read-only (and only it, and its attachments, never through a link) with the text of archive-only messages read without unarchiving; new messages carried as sealed pages with a catch-up place, edits and unsends, 30 days first; replies sent through Messages with a fixed compiled script taking the text as data, answered with the message's guid; open at login; Ed25519-signed updates (none without MA's key; Developer ID checked once a team is set); "Remove MA Connector" unpairs and removes everything it kept. `scripts/build-app.sh` (both architectures, the bundle by hand, ad-hoc with the hardened runtime, no local path or name in the binary), `scripts/sign-notarize.sh` (MA's Developer ID and notarization only), `connector-harness`, `connector-tool`, `connector-shots` (never in the app).
- **Added (courier-envelope):** `DeviceEnvelope`, the `device` kind: the device id in the aad both ways, its own signing domain, 32 KiB envelopes.
- **Added (deskd `courier/imsg/connector/`):** the seam's `connector` path: `courier.imsg.pair {code}` (claims the code, pins the Mac's keys in the sealed sign-in, one Mac a desk), pages into the seam's sink with their place, gaps answered with `resume`, sends answered with their guid and recorded before the echo, `bye` and the platform's forgetting on Remove, a device no account names forgotten, iMessage switched off keeping nothing, oversized pages refused.
- **Changed (deskd relay):** the drain hands `device` envelopes to `DeviceInbox` (and runs for a desk with no address but a paired Mac); `notify relay {reason: devices}`; `RelayApi` gains the claim, the desk's own keys and the device delete. **Changed (seam):** `ImsgPath.removed()`, called by `ImsgTransport.removing`.
- **Added (the Desk app):** Courier › Accounts › iMessage on your Mac: the code from the Mac, the check number to compare, the Mac's row (connected, waiting, needs you, disconnected) with Disconnect; the mock desk's Mac.
- **Added (test kit):** the fake relay's device routes and `FakeMac`.
- **Tests:** Swift 32 (the envelope vectors byte for byte with Java, the drop, signing, Messages fixtures, the bridge against an in-memory platform and desk); deskd `MacConnectorTest` 12; `MacHarnessE2ETest` with `connectors/mac/scripts/e2e-fake-relay.sh` (the Swift app's bridge on a Mac against deskd's path through the fake relay); vitest `mac.test.ts` 5; Playwright `couriermac.shell.spec.ts` 4 (three themes, axe). Security: stand-in read `~/work/maia-mode/mac-connector/security.md`: MC-M1, MC-M2, MC-L1 to MC-L3 and MC-I1, MC-I3, MC-I4 fixed.

## Unreleased - Courier's 7-day staging soak (CR-4.1; `sub/mm-courier-soak`; test code only, in no release)
- **Added (deskd test sources):** `CourierStagingSoak`, two staging test desks of deskd's own Courier module, store, keys, relay transport and `courier-send` on the real staging relay and door. Each signs as a self-hosted test desk, with sessions renewed by `SessionImds` from its pairing key. Setup and teardown: `services/deskd/e2e/courier-soak.mjs`. Unattended: `courier-soak-watchdog.sh` from cron.

- Status and daily results: ma-platform `docs/sub-status/courier-soak.md`.
## Unreleased - Helpers on MA's credits (`sub/mm-helpers-provider`; deskd, desk-agent and the Agent app; ma-platform contract 0.4.8, ADR 0142; in no release)
The owner, Nov 15: MA acts as the AI provider for members' own desk agents too, so the welcome never stalls on "bring your own key". API.md §24.
- **deskd:** `agent/HelperModel` relays `helpers_model` and `helpers_credits` tcalls to the Platform's `/v1/desk-helpers/call` and `/credits` on Desk Maia's service, with her proof (`DeskMaiaClient.call(route, body)`); refuses Desk Maia, a Guild Desk, a teen's desk, no service, other fields, over 3 MiB and more than 4 at once; the hello says `helpers: {model: true}` while the service is configured.
- **desk-agent:** provider `ma-credits` (`provider/ma/HelperServiceProvider`), key `KeyRing.CREDITS` (no key material), model `auto`; `LlmGateway` lets any of the member's own agents use it on that key only, never Desk Maia, and keeps no dollars for it; `agent.key.list` shows the member's credits as a first row while offered, `agent.key.models` one model, no delete; `agent.bind` and `agent.create` take it; a new agent with no key goes on the credits when the member has some. `DeskLink.helpersModel`, the socket's hello `helpers`.

## Unreleased - Desk Maia in credits, never money (`sub/mm-maia-credits`; desk-agent and the Desk app; ma-platform contract 0.4.8 `desk-maia.md`; in no release)
The owner, Nov 15: members never see dollar amounts for Maia; her allowance is in credits, its day and week amounts pace only, and past the month's credits and top-ups she keeps working on a lighter model and says so.
- **Changed (desk-agent):** her calls are the company's: the gateway and the ledger keep no dollar figure of hers (her reply's `costMicroUsd` is null), her rows never count on the desk's or a key's limits (the $20 desk month included), and no dollar limit binds her, only her run's steps. Every request asks for credits (`units: "credits"`, contract 0.4.8, Security's MC-M1), so answers carry no `costMicroUsd`; each answer's `costCredits`, `allowance.credits`, `drawingFrom` and `lighter` are read (none of them needed). deskd's `MaiaModel` allows `units`; the Platform's 0.4.8 ships first, then deskd, then desk-agent. Her note says her credits, where they're drawing from, top-up credits and the lighter model ("say so plainly once, keep helping"), never a dollar figure; the day-limit stop line is gone. The member's own cap on her is `caps.coordinatorDailyCredits` (none by default) against her summed `costCredits`, refusing her turns `limit` `limit_cap` until the desk's midnight; `coordinatorDailyUsd` is retired (ignored, dropped at the next change, refused if sent). A null step cost no longer throws in `ManagerAgent`.
- **Changed (the Desk app, Maia Mode's room):** nothing of her own usage in dollars (the topic's `spend` and the foot's fields for it are gone; "Briefs today ... · your keys", her briefs' cost on the member's own agents, stays); the topic's `allowance` shows "Maia credits: 1,550 of 2,500 left · renews December 1", where she's drawing from once today's share is used, top-up credits, "Your Maia credits are used up, so Maia is on a lighter model until December 1.", and a day cap the member set, as a date and never a countdown. New `limit_cap` and "renews" lines in `allowanceLine`. The To-do's `cap` alert is in credits ("Maia has used 80% of the 200 credits you set for today"), and the team view's `maia` row carries `spendCredits` with `spendMicroUsd` 0.
- **Proof:** `docs/sub-status/mm-maia-company.md`, "Credits (Nov 15)".

## Unreleased - Courier's iMessage and iPhone texts through the member's own Mac, behind one `imsg` seam (`sub/mm-courier-imessage`, the Courier launch plan's Wave 2.6; deskd and the test kit; Courier still behind `COURIER_READY`, in no release)
The owner, Nov 15: MA builds its own signed Mac connector as the main path; BlueBubbles Server stays as a second path, both behind one seam (`docs/sub-status/imsg-seam.md`). Draft ADR `draft-courier-imessage`; API.md §21, "iMessage on the member's Mac".
- **Added (deskd `courier/imsg/`):** `ImsgTransport` (one iMessage account a desk, sealed like a linked mailbox's sign-in, its path picked by the sealed settings, the queue's `imessage` and `sms` senders, the chat a reply goes to, `imessage-off` and `imessage-send-off`), `ImsgSink` (a Mac's messages into the store the same way for every path: one thread per person, texts as `sms`, group chats as one conversation each, reactions, edits, unsends and group changes as words, files through `desk-parse` into the raw blob so Save to Files and the danger rules are email's, the member's own messages as outgoing, backfill), `ImsgPath`, `ImsgSend`, `ImsgMessage`.
- **Added (deskd `courier/imsg/bluebubbles/`):** the member's BlueBubbles Server, out from the desk at the address they give: HTTPS only through `Net`'s address checks, the JDK's trust or a certificate pinned on first connect at their own address (never a tunnel's), a changed one refused before the password goes until the member accepts it; catch-up from a cursor with a 20-minute overlap, the socket as a doorbell, sends by AppleScript with the echo stored once, the Mac's Contacts for names and card suggestions; limits against a hostile server (sizes, a wall clock per exchange, paced looks, 20,000 new messages a day).
- **Added (methods):** `courier.accounts.add {kind: imessage, server, secret}`, the Mac in `courier.accounts.list` (`sync`: state, mode, through, certificate, socket), `courier.accounts.signin` for the Mac (`acceptCertificate`), `courier.accounts.remove`, and `courier.accounts.check` ("Check again").
- **Changed (deskd store):** `courier.db` version 6 (`imessage_chats`, `imessage_sent`, `cards`, `card_endpoints`); `Courier.Inbound.group`; the queue reaches an iMessage person by number or by the Apple ID they last wrote from; `courier.links.suggest` offers the Mac's cards first (`source: mac`, `strength: card`); `senderConfirmed` is true for an iMessage and false for a text; `Net.openTcp` (a public IP literal and the caller's ports).
- **Changed (test kit):** the fake BlueBubbles serves HTTPS and WSS, `GET message/{guid}`, `GET contact`, unsend and the member's own messages.
- **Tests:** `ImessageTest` 13 (connect and seal, one thread across iMessage and texts, Requests, sleep and wake with 120 messages, words, groups, files into Files, sending with a retry and the echo once, contacts, the pinned certificate, a changed one refused, the switch, sign in again, the daily cap), `HostileMacTest` 8, `ImsgSinkTest` 10. Security: stand-in read, one High and two Mediums fixed. Status: `docs/sub-status/mm-courier-imessage.md`.

In Light it's now `border.strong` (3.7:1 at the default; `src/theme/rangeTrack.test.ts` holds 3:1 at every Contrast from the default up, at every Blue). Zen and Dark unchanged.

## Unreleased - Desk Maia: terse, calm and gentle, said once, gliding in (`sub/mm-maia-voice`; desk-agent and the Desk app; ma-platform `sub/mm-maia-voice` for her instructions)
The owner's first real use, Nov 15: he typed "Hi Maia. I'm new around here" and got two welcomes in one reply, a list of what she does, "Maia Mode's settings" and "switched on": "She's supposed to be TERSE, CALM, and GENTLE. This was the most stressful response ever.... Also, the words didn't glide in, fade in and write naturally". Status: `docs/sub-status/mm-maia-voice.md`.
- **Fixed (desk-agent):** the doubled reply. Her run's loop adds each model step's words to her one reply, and after her `ask_member` it called the model again, which wrote the reply again. Her choices now end the run (`ManagerAgent.Tools.endsRun`, passed through `AgentTools`, `AgentService` and `Memories`; `MaiaProfile`), a reply with no words takes her question, and `maia/Restated` cuts any later step to what's new before it's shown (her service answers a step whole). Her rare longer part (after a `<more>` line) never repeats her short reply.
- **Changed (desk-agent):** her context's no-desks line no longer names "Maia Mode's settings" or says "switched on" (no such setting exists yet): she doesn't raise it, and if asked for desk work says gently she can't reach their desks from here yet. `ask_member`'s description and result say her reply ends there. Her voice itself is the Platform's instructions (ma-platform `deskPrompt.ts`).
- **Added (the Desk app):** her words glide in word by word (`maiamode/glide.ts`, `Say.tsx`): about 11 words a second with a breath after a sentence, started 120 ms after they arrive and paced on their own clock, so bursts never show; each word fades and rises 0.3 em over 460 ms (opacity and transform only). Her reply's space opens to fit its words by one height transition, and grows the same way if more come; her dots open and close their space; a new line's gap opens with it; her choices fade in 160 ms after her last word and fold away once answered. Her longer part folds under a quiet "More" (a chevron, "Less" when open; `aria-expanded`, inert while folded). Reduced motion: none of it, the words are simply there. A line already there when the room opens never glides (`RoomMessage.live`).
- **Tests:** desk-agent `RestatedTest` 9 and 5 new `MaiaModeFlowTest` cases (the doubled welcome and the question-only reply fail without the fix); vitest `glide.test.ts` 7 and a `deskSource` case; Playwright `maiaGlide.shell.spec.ts` 11 (Light, Zen and Dark, desktop and phone, a 4x slower CPU: 0 long frames, the frame gap 17 ms at most, the line above moving 4.5 px a frame at most on a desktop and 11 on a phone, her choices after her last word; reduced motion; More and Less by pointer and keyboard with axe); `maiaMode.shell.spec.ts` 17/17. Real-model voice evals: `services/desk-agent/docs/maia-evals.md`, "Her voice".
## Unreleased - Google's Gemini and Moonshot AI's Kimi with a member's own keys, direct (`sub/mm-provider-compat`; desk-agent, the image's name filter and the Agent app; in no release)
The owner, Nov 15: his own Gemini and Kimi keys on a desk agent, not through OpenRouter. desk-agent `README.md`, "Google and Moonshot AI"; API.md §11.
- **Added (desk-agent): `google` and `moonshot`**, one `provider/chat/ChatCompletionsProvider` on each provider's own OpenAI-compatible Chat Completions (`https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/openai`, `https://api.moonshot.ai/v1`; `DESK_AGENT_GOOGLE_URL`, `DESK_AGENT_MOONSHOT_URL`, `off` as a kill switch; ON by default): streamed with `include_usage`, the key check and model list from `GET {base}/models` (Gemini's 400 for a bad key is `key_refused`; `models/` comes off its ids; non-chat models left out, newest family first), Gemini's thought signatures and Moonshot's `reasoning_content` sent back to their own provider only, Moonshot's top-level `cached_tokens`, Gemini's pictures. Not in the price table yet, so calls count at its highest (ADR 0043).
- **Changed (desk-agent):** `ChatCompletions` moved to `provider/chat` with OpenRouter's stream reader, which OpenRouter now shares (its behaviour unchanged); calls without `index` are told apart; a call's id and name are cut at 128 characters and a step's text is capped. The key-prefix check also reads OpenAI's `sk-proj-`, `sk-svcacct-` and `sk-admin-`, as Moonshot shares `sk-`.
- **Changed (image):** `agent-egress.conf` lists `generativelanguage.googleapis.com` and `api.moonshot.ai` (ADR 0070's addendum of Nov 15); `F-UNIT` six names, `F-PROV`, `F-PLACE`, Google's `F-CF` lines, and `desk-agent-filter/conf_test.go` on the shipped list. The filter's binary and pin are unchanged; a desk gets the names with the next bake.
- **Fixed (the Agent app):** a key whose prefix two providers share (OpenAI's and Moonshot AI's `sk-`) goes through under either, and under any other the line names both (`keyOwners`). The mock lists both providers.
- **Tests:** desk-agent full suite 1581, 0 failures (3 skipped), with `ChatCompletionsProviderTest` and `OwnKeyChatSocketTest`; Go filter tests; vitest 414; Playwright `key.spec.ts` 5. Security: stand-in read CLEAR, one Low and three Infos fixed. Brand review: pass. Status: `docs/sub-status/mm-provider-compat.md`.

- **Security:** stand-in read (`~/work/maia-mode/phase2/security.md`): MA-M1 fixed (the Platform's word reaches desk-agent; check-ins capped), MA-L2, MA-L3, MA-I2 fixed here.

## Unreleased - Desk Maia's wallet: she plans her briefs around what desk agents cost, and asks only when it matters (`sub/mm-maia-budget`; desk-agent and the Desk app; in no release)
The owner, Nov 15: "People aren't going to want to use and trust Maia Mode if it just destroys their wallets", then "Will users have to approve that EVERY time?" API.md §23, "Her briefs and the member's wallet".
- **Added (desk-agent): `maia/Wallet`.** Before her `desk_message` goes, code estimates it (the answering agent's usual task from its desk's own ledger, times the brief's size, 1 to 6, from code) and holds it only when it would pass that agent's own limit (`maia_limit`, never waived), take her briefs past the member's daily budget for them ($3 by default), or cost more than 3 times that agent's usual task and more than the ask line ($0.50) (`maia_held`). A default day of ordinary briefs asks nothing. Held, code posts its own card in her thread with the estimate (`choices.kind: "budget"`, `cost.line`): Go, "Always OK for tasks like this", Cheaper way, Skip; at a limit Cheaper way, Try anyway, Skip, or with nothing left Another way and Skip. Go lets one brief through for 15 minutes; "Always OK" stops that agent's tasks of that size from asking again; Skip starts nothing. Her note lists each desk's answering agent, its usual task, what's left today and this month and what one brief may spend, and her briefs against the budget; her context says to pick the cheapest desk that can do the job and trim or split to fit. `{maia}.budget` and `agent.maia.set {budget}` (the member's own; "Always OK" only narrows there). Audit `maia.brief.held`, `maia.brief.answer`, `maia.budget.always`.
- **Added (desk-agent): the team view's headroom** (`agent.usage.summary`): each agent's `headroom` (what's left of its day and month limits, its run limit, what a brief from another desk may spend there, its usual task over tasks and over her briefs), `maiaBriefs` (her briefs' runs and spend on this desk, today and this month) and, on the home desk, `maia` (her briefs across the desks with the budget). `UsageLedger.spendOfRuns`, `Team.briefCap`. The room's topic gains `briefs` and the foot line "Briefs today $1.20 of $3".
- **Changed (the Desk app, Maia Mode's room):** the office reads each desk's team view when it connects and when an agent there stops working (at most every 20 s), and sends each agent's `budget` and each desk's `briefs` in `agent.maia.observe`. The To-do foot shows "Briefs today $1.20 of $3 · your keys" (the member's own agents on their own keys), which opens the same per desk, today and this month; Maia's own usage is no longer shown there in dollars (the owner, Nov 15: members see her usage in credits, which the company-account work brings). Code's card shows its estimate as a quiet line ("About $1.20 · usually $0.20 · Work Lead").
- **Unchanged:** every desk's hard caps (ADR 0043's reserve, the run cap, the share for desk messages) refuse as before; she has no tool that changes a budget, an ask line, "Always OK", a cap or a limit.
- **Tests:** desk-agent `WalletTest` 15, `TeamViewHeadroomTest` 2, `MaiaWalletEvalTest` 3; vitest maiamode 82. Security: stand-in read, one Medium and one Low fixed and confirmed. Brand review: pass. Status: `docs/sub-status/mm-maia-budget.md`.

## Unreleased - Desk Maia's prompt caching and one cap (`sub/mm-maia-cache`; deskd and desk-agent; ma-platform contract 0.4.6 `desk-maia.md`; in no release)
- **Changed (desk-agent):** her `context` is byte-stable across a session (the choice rule without the weight, her desks and grants, memory, the desk's tool rules); the time, her choice weight, briefs left, her allowance as her service last reported it ("Maia allowance left: 64% (resets 5:00 pm).") and any cap line go in a new `note` (`RunSetup.note`, `ChatRequest.note`), which `MaiaServiceProvider` sends as the contract's `note` (at most 2,000 characters) and her service puts after the thread, outside every cached prefix. With no `maiaMode.coordinatorDailyUsd` from the Platform the desk sets no coordinator top of its own (100, was Officer's former cap); a member's lower cap still binds. `AnthropicProvider` caches the thread too: a second breakpoint on its last block, a note after it. **Changed (deskd):** `maia_model` passes `note`. The release order is the Platform's 0.4.6, then deskd, then desk-agent. Status: `docs/sub-status/mm-maia-company.md`, "Caching (Nov 15)".

## Unreleased - Desk Maia runs on the company account (`sub/mm-maia-company`; deskd, desk-agent and the Desk app; ma-platform contract 0.4.4 `desk-maia.md`; in no release)
The owner, Nov 14: "Maia ALWAYS runs on the company account." Members never add a key for Maia; their desk agents keep their own keys.
- **Added (deskd): Desk Maia's model** (API.md §23, "Her model"): a `maia_model` tcall from desk-agent goes to her service, never to the tool host. `DeskMaiaClient` sends `POST {endpoints.maia}/v1/desk-maia/call` with `{"v": 1, …}` and a fresh `X-MA-Desk-Auth` per call (the gateway's desk proof, also signing `x-ma-maia-id` and `x-ma-body-sha256`; `DeskAuth.forMaia`), one role refresh on `401 desk_auth`, 150 s, answers over 2 MiB unread, nothing of a call logged. `MaiaModel` refuses first by deskd's own facts (not `maia`, a Guild Desk, not an adult, Free, no service, fields outside the contract's, over 3 MiB, more than 4 at once) and passes the service's `code` and its `reason`, `resetsAt`, `window`, `retryAfterSec`. `PlatformConfig` reads `endpoints.maia` (https only) and `endpoints.maiaId`; the tools hello says `maia: {model: true}` while both are there, and is sent again when they first appear.
- **Added (desk-agent): her company provider.** `ProviderId.MA` and `provider/ma/MaiaServiceProvider`: the task (`turn`, `step` after tool results, `notes`), the desk's context and the thread, never a model; files as a line naming them; the answer streamed once with the service's `costMicroUsd`; every refusal a clean run end (`LIMIT`, `TOO_LARGE`, `FORBIDDEN` are new `ProviderException` kinds, with `Details`); after a `429 limit` nothing is sent until it resets. `KeyRing.COMPANY` (`ma-maia`) lent as an empty lease, in no list; `LlmGateway` reserves her calls at a fixed worst case and settles at the service's cost, and refuses `ma` to any other agent. `DeskLink.maiaModel()` and `Reply.details` from the hello and `terr`.
- **Changed (desk-agent):** her binding is always the company one (set when her row is made and at every start; `agent.bind` for her is `forbidden` `company`); `agent.providers` doesn't list `ma` and `agent.key.add` refuses it; her sends are `unavailable` `no_service` until deskd offers her model, and `precondition_failed` `disclosure` while the desk holds only `maia-mode-v1`; `MaiaMode.DISCLOSURE` is `maia-mode-v2`; `MaiaProfile.PROMPT` is gone (her Maia Mode instructions are the Platform's): her context is the desk's dynamic part only; her note-taker is the `notes` task; a reply that ended on her allowance carries `limit: {reason, window, resetsAt}`; her thread is compacted after `too_large`. **Her memory** keeps Support Maia's rules (`memory/MaiaMemory`, the Platform's private check ported): nothing private saved, and older private lines never pinned. Other agents unchanged.
- **Changed (the Desk app, Maia Mode's room):** no key flow at all (the `no_key` line, "Use {Lead}'s key", `agent.bind` of maia and the queue of refused messages are gone); one plain line when her allowance is used up, with the reset in the member's own time ("You’ve used today’s Maia allowance. It resets tomorrow at 5:00 PM."; week, month, trial and the shared allowance too), no button; "Maia isn’t available right now. Try again in a few minutes."; the disclosure is version 2 with the new "What goes to an AI provider", and the room shows it again when the desk holds only v1.
- **Proof:** `docs/sub-status/mm-maia-company.md`.

- **Added:** two sliders under Theme (the owner, Nov 14; his pick of the Designer's sheet: blue range A, grey to today's, and the sheet's contrast range), in Light, Zen and Dark, with Reset on the Theme line. One setting for the account, shared with the Forums (ma-platform contract `appearance.md`): `/v1/me` `appearance`, `PUT /v1/me/appearance` when the slider rests. ## Unreleased - Courier's office pieces finished: Download, previews and archive lists; one person across channels in deskd; flow 7's note; one name for comparing; the link's own fingerprint; courier-send's uploads (`sub/mm-courier-finish`; the Desk app, deskd and the test kit; behind `COURIER_READY = false`)
- **Added: Download, Show and Show files** (`web/src/apps/courier/bulk.ts`, `Message.tsx`): an attachment's bytes, its redrawn picture and an archive's list over the desk's own `bulk`, kept only when every byte matches the desk's SHA-256 and trailer; a dangerous type and a refused message's "Download the message" ask first, in place; nothing in Spam or Trash is fetched. deskd: `courier.attachment.stop`, and `courier.message.raw` of a refused message needs `allowDanger` (`danger: unread`).
- **Added: `courier.links.*` in deskd** (`courier/store/Links.java`, `courier.db` version 5): suggest, link, undo, unlink, dismiss and lookup as the office proposed (API.md §21), and `source` on a contact's endpoints. Never a silent merge: nothing on arrival links, a refused link writes nothing, a blocked address can't be linked, and the office no longer merges in a link's place on an older deskd.

Share holds the link until the directory has the keys.

Now `install` to `.volume.json.new` and `mv -fT` over, as desk. Data was never at risk; the record misreported. Record `docs/sub-status/volume-record-copy.md`.

Anthropic lists its models newest first, so the New agent form, the key screen and "Use Lead's key" put every new agent, and Maia, on `claude-fable-5-2`, which the owner had stopped on Nov 11.

## Unreleased - The Browser's zoom no longer waits behind tabs that don't answer (`sub/mm-release5-zoom`, found by Maia Mode release 5's gate; deskd only)
- **Fixed (deskd): a Browser zoom set from the office could wait minutes.** On every connection after the first (once the sweep was done), the check on connect read each member tab's zoom (`Page.getLayoutMetrics`) under `applying`, at the full request timeout (5 s) each. A tab Chromium restored but hasn't loaded has no renderer to answer, so on the staging TEST desk (52 tabs) every `browser.zoom.set` waited until Chromium stopped: the slider moved, nothing changed on the desk, and the level landed a minute after the Browser closed. Now a tab gets at most 1 s (`BrowserZoom.TAB_READ`), the check reads tabs for at most 3 s in all (`TABS_READ`, the rest are read at their next change, as a tab that hangs already was), and `applying` is a fair lock, so a waiting set goes next. Nothing in the interface changes (API.md §10, "Zoom").
- **Proof:** `BrowserZoomTest.tabsThatNeverAnswerDontHoldASetBehindTheConnectsCheck` (no tab answers a read after a restart; the set answers within the budget and lands) fails on `97c937fd` (no reply) and passes here; `docs/sub-status/mm-release5-zoom.md`.
