## Unreleased - The office says when a desk was stopped for its traffic (TD-162; `sub/traffic-stop-line`, in no release; the words are Sales' final words)
- **Added:** a desk the Platform's cost guard stopped for its traffic (`stopReason: "traffic"`, `stopDetail: {scope, clearsAt}`, contract 0.3.91) says why where Start would be, in the Browser and Agent windows: "This desk was stopped because its internet traffic went far beyond normal personal use." (or, for a shared machine, "...for a while because of very heavy internet traffic on the computer it shares with other desks."), "You can start it again after 3:40 PM today." (tomorrow; on a later day "You can start it again on Nov 14."; the viewer's zone) and a quiet "Contact support". No Start before `clearsAt`, and the office sends none (`wakeDesk` and a wake queued before the link); after it, the usual wake. While stopping, the same reason and time are in the stopping notice, and the desk's strip says "stopped for heavy traffic". No number from `limit` anywhere. The Management Console shows no stop reason today, so it is unchanged.
- **Tests:** `src/desk/trafficStop.test.ts`; `tests/e2e/trafficStop.shell.spec.ts` (`link=real`, three themes at 1280 and 390, axe, host scope, stopping, after `clearsAt`, data guard unchanged).

## Unreleased - The saved layout's mirror goes on every signed-out state (`sub/lsub-iss67-layout-wipe`, ISS-67; privacy; nothing a member reads changes)
- **Fixed (privacy):** this browser's copy of the account's saved layout (`ma.layout.v2`, the old `ma.layout.v1`, the record of what hasn't reached Platform `ma.layout.sync.v1`, the free tier's and the setup circle's prefs mirrors, and the background's) was never wiped: the next person on a shared computer could read the last member's desk ids, apps, arrangement, styles and preferences. Every key now goes on Sign out and Sign out everywhere (after their last words save the layout), `/signed-out`, a start with nobody signed in, a failed `/auth/callback`, a dead session (a refused refresh, or "Stay signed in" run out) whether it goes to sign in or ends on the card, a 401 a refresh can't mend, a refused (or closed) account, and another tab's sign-out or refused refresh, which this tab hears over the token's BroadcastChannel. One copy covers Personal, Work and Guild desks, whatever the scope.
- **Fixed:** a page that has signed out never writes the mirror again (the debounce, a flush as it goes, the keepalive save), and nothing writes it while no refresh token is kept in this browser, so a tab another tab signed out can't write it back.
- **Fixed (per account):** the mirror carries whose it is (`ma.layout.owner.v1`, the token's `sub`). A sign-in as anyone else, at `/auth/callback` over a kept copy or in another tab, wipes another account's copy from storage and takes the last account's layout out of the tab's memory, so it can't draw, be written back, or go up into the new account's empty document. A copy from before this build is the first account's.
- **Fixed (Security's ISS67-L1):** the free tier's and the setup circle's prefs mirrors are written only while the layout's mirror may be, so a stale tab can't write them back after a sign-out.
- **Changed:** a dead session's unsaved arrangement (`ma.layout.sync.v1`) no longer waits through the trip to sign in for the same member; it goes with the rest. Platform's document stands.
- **Tests:** `web/src/platform/session.signedOut.test.ts` (every path, and a refresh that fails for now keeps it), `web/src/state/layoutMirror.test.ts` (the keys, the hold, the token rule, per account), `web/src/platform/tokenStore.test.ts` (another tab's token going); `web/tests/e2e/layoutWipe.shell.spec.ts`. Twelve specs that seed a mirror now seed it on the first load that's signed in. Status: `docs/sub-status/lsub-iss67-layout-wipe.md`.

## Unreleased - The LS-29 Notebook spec waits for ProseMirror's selection before Backspace (`sub/lsub-nb706`; test only, in no release)
- **Tests:** `web/tests/e2e/notebook.shell.spec.ts`'s LS-29 test ("a page whose Title line is empty…") failed 15 of 100 runs alone on `main`, and 19 of 100 at 1 CPU. Its Backspace often reached ProseMirror before Chromium's `selectionchange` from Shift+End. ProseMirror still held Control+Home's caret at the start of the Title, so it stopped the key and nothing was deleted. The test now waits for ProseMirror's own selection to be "Draft" (`selectedText`) before Backspace, as `rt-notebook.mjs` waits for its caret. After the fix: 100 of 100 at 6 and at 1 CPU, 60 of 60 at 0.5. No product change, no sleep, no timeout raised.
- Status: `docs/sub-status/lsub-nb706.md`.

## Unreleased - Signing in again revokes the session it replaces (ISS-68 follow-up, as `ma-platform`'s Platform app; `sub/lsub-iss68-desk`; UI/UX approved, Security cleared with no findings; rides Desk app release 15)
- **Fixed:** `office.`'s `/auth/callback` (`web/src/platform/auth.ts`, `finishSignIn`), for managed login and Google: a sign-in over a stored session left the old refresh token alive at Cognito. Now the old one is read, the new one written and read back under `ma-refresh`, and only then is the old one sent to the existing `revokeRefreshToken`, not awaited and never an error. Nothing for the same token, a first sign-in, a failed one, an answer with no refresh token, or a write the browser refuses (that still fails the sign-in, as before). `underRefreshLock` now passes back its work's result. Nothing a member sees changes. The hand-off landing (`/_signin/handoff`, `ma-platform` `web/signin`) already revoked what it replaced.
- **Tests:** `src/platform/auth.test.ts` 11 new (5 fail on `main`), `tests/e2e/release.spec.ts` 7 new. Status `docs/sub-status/lsub-iss68-desk.md`.

## Unreleased - The bars' short-viewport mode: one row each under 400 px high (the accessibility audit's finding 6, the Product Designer's spec; `sub/lsub-desk-bar-400`, Lead Subcontractor for the UI/UX Engineer)
- **Fixed:** at 400 % zoom the top and bottom bars wrapped to several rows, overlapped each other and covered the desks, and at 320 x 200 Previous desk and Next desk took no press. Under 400 CSS px high, at any width, each bar is now one 44 px row. Top: the mark, then the scope switch as one menu button naming the current scope ("All" and a chevron) on the left; the shield, full screen, the bell and the account on the right, 36 px each with 4 px gaps (8 px with a finger, so each keeps a whole 44 px target, invisibly). Bottom: the desk switch, the zoom as one button showing the level ("83%"), whose popover has −, + and Fit with their keys, then the circles. The desks keep 96 px of 320 x 200 and 121 of 360 x 225, and the bars never touch. Above 400 px nothing changes.
- **The menus:** the scope list is the office's menu (Personal, Work, Guild, All, the current one checked), `aria-haspopup="menu"`; Up and Down move, Enter or Space picks, Esc and Tab shut it, and the keyboard goes back to its button. The zoom popover is `aria-haspopup="dialog"`; Left and Right move, and `+`, `=`, `-`, `_` and `0` work while it has the keyboard. The canvas's own keys are unchanged and still act only while the canvas has focus. Both panels are in the popovers chunk (`chrome/BarPanels.tsx`), the buttons in `chrome/BarMenus.tsx`; `useIsShort` (`canvas/phone.ts`) and `app.css` share the query.
- **At every size:** the top bar's `.top-actions` group takes no press of its own (`pointer-events: none`, its controls `auto`), so the empty wrapper never eats a press.
- **Tests:** `reflow.shell.spec.ts`'s `test.fixme` for finding 6 is a test now, with the short mode at 320 x 200 and 360 x 225 in three themes (rows, gaps, canvas, presses, both menus open on screen, axe), the scope menu's keys, the zoom popover's buttons and keys, the canvas's keys in short mode, the 400 px edge both ways, `.top-actions` letting presses through at four sizes, 44 px targets with a finger, and 1440 x 900 as before. Status: `STATUS.md`.

## Unreleased - The office sets and clears the landing's signed-in cookie (S-148, `sub/signed-in-cookie`, UI/UX Engineer; in no release)
- **Added:** where client-config carries `signedInCookie` (prod only, ma-platform contract `auth.md` §3.4), the office sets `ma_signed_in=1` on the parent domain for the member's "Stay signed in" when a sign-in finishes, on each refresh and when the choice is read, and clears it on Sign out, Sign out everywhere and a refused refresh. The landing then shows "Your office" in place of "Sign in" for a member who is signed in. Nothing a member reads in the office changes; without the key nothing is written. `web/src/platform/signedInCookie.ts`. Status: `docs/sub-status/signed-in-cookie.md`.

- **Fixed (tests):** `maia.shell.spec.ts`'s whole-turn test checked Send's `aria-disabled`, the "Looking that up…" line and her thinking state while a stub turn of about 0.7 s was running, and free Maia's slow-start test checked her calm line inside a 2.5 s wait, so a loaded machine could look too late. ## Unreleased - Files downloads are always `application/octet-stream` (`sub/files-blob-octet`; Security Low from the ADR 0054 read)
- **Changed:** the Files download that goes through the browser's blob storage (no save-file picker) now makes its blob `application/octet-stream` whatever the file's type, so no blob in the office ever carries a script type from desk data. The `download` attribute still keeps the file name. `BlobSink.blob()` no longer takes a type and `openTarget` no longer takes one (`web/src/apps/files/save.ts`, `engine.ts`). ## Unreleased - Security's open Lows for the desk image and the shared-host bake (`sub/lsub-seclows-system`; the image recipe and the host bake, in no image)
- **Fixed (image, links out):** the `xdg-open` stand-in refuses a URL with any control byte (below 0x20, or DEL), not only CR and LF, before anything reaches deskd's socket (Security's image-035 Low 2). `services/deskd/desk-tools/xdg-open` and the recipe's copy, byte for byte.
- **Fixed (image):** `desk-browser.service` is ordered after `desk-egress.service`, so the Browser never runs unshaped at boot, whoever starts it (NET031-5). Only ordering: a desk without the shaper still gets a Browser.
- **Fixed (host bake):** a role without `kms:GetPublicKey` on `alias/ma-host-template` stops the bake, unless `ALLOW_NO_KMS_COMPARE=1` (ADR 0077 Low 1). A release bake (`ma-shared-host-`) refuses a changed or untracked file under `image/` (Low 2). The host's batch key is held to the desk image's copy in the template, with no way round it; the old check compared with a staged copy the desk recipe had already removed, so it never ran (Low 3).
- Status: `docs/sub-status/lsub-seclows-system.md`.

## Unreleased - The Notebook: a search opened right after a page keeps its box, and the demo's picker says Zen (`sub/lsub-nb-search-flake`; the Notebook module, in no release)
- **Fixed:** a page opened from search or the Temporal view, or a new page, asked for the cursor twice: TipTap's own autofocus and `PageEditor`'s effect. TipTap's focus command calls `view.focus()` a frame after it's asked, wherever the focus is by then, so the second one landed a frame after the page had the cursor and took it back from anything opened meanwhile: a search opened straight after lost its box, and what was typed and Esc went to the page. The effect is now the only autofocus (`web/notebook/src/ui/Editor.tsx`). This was the flake in `notebook.spec.ts`'s "search with Ctrl/Cmd+K" at its last line: 7 of 40 runs failed on `main` `99c2af33`, 0 of 100 with the fix.
- **Changed (demo):** the Notebook demo's theme picker says "Zen", as the office does (ADR 0093). The id stays `dusk` (`data-theme`, the saved choice, `?theme=dusk`, CSS), so a saved choice still holds. `notebook.spec.ts`, `colorMenus.spec.ts` and `tests/perf/record.mjs` pick "Zen"; `media/notebook-demo.mp4` was recorded before and still shows Dusk. Status: `docs/sub-status/lsub-nb-search-flake.md`.

## Unreleased - Desk apps: an Apps sheet per desk, one tap to add, and each app's own window (S-97's Desk app half, ADR 0054; `sub/apps-host`; the Desk app, not released, and nothing to add until deskd's apps are live on a desk)
- **Added:** "Apps" in a desk's menu opens that desk's Apps sheet: each app with its glyph, name, one line and "Free", and Add to desk. A row opens the app's details: what it does, who made it, "Reviewed by Minimalist Alliance" (or "By Minimalist Alliance" for our own), what it asks for in plain words ("It asks for nothing else: no internet, no files, no Browser."), and its size. Add to desk is the one tap, with no card to confirm.
- **Added:** an added app is a tile on its desk, at the first free spot, showing what the app says (its count, its last verse); a stopped app's tile says why ("Couldn't start", "Stopped: used too much memory"), and its window has Restart. Open opens it in its own window, in the carousel, on a phone full screen, or live in its enlarged tile.
- **Added:** Remove takes the app off the desk and keeps what it saved: add it again and it's all there. On a Guild Desk only the Founder adds and removes; members open.
- **Added:** on a phone, an Apps button at the end of the carousel's thumb row opens the Apps sheet full screen.
- **Added:** the Bible's window wears its own colors (Lapis), its view too, in Light, Zen and Dark; a view can copy to the clipboard from a tap of its own, and can never read it; the keyboard shortcuts list has a Bible group where the Bible is on the desk.
- **Security:** an app's window opens only after its files are checked against Minimalist Alliance's review signature (our own built-in apps against their file hashes), in a sandbox with no network; anything off and it says "This app couldn't be opened safely." and shows nothing. A window that holds up the office is paused ("Paused to keep your desk smooth", with Resume).
- Kill switches: client-config `deskApps: { enabled: false }`, and deskd's own. The office's CSP needs `blob:` in `script-src` for app windows (Platform). Status: `docs/sub-status/apps-host.md`.

## Unreleased - ADR 0036's test 1 on a shared host, on host 0.1.11 (TD-223; `sub/guild-td223-shared`; test tooling and results only)
- **Added:** `image/lab/td223/rowmerge.py` (the repeats merged for `test1_shared_judge.py`); `lab.sh tools`, `FLOOD_EARLY` (B13 under the flood) and `LEAD`; `h17.sh`'s `SA_PURPOSE`, `SA_SLOTS` and `SA_VIA_CLIENT` (the host driven through the client on its private address, one long-lived connection per end). Nothing in an image, a release or a service changes.
- **Results** (ADR 0036, the addendum of 2026-11-09's result of 2026-11-11, host 0.1.11, six shaped slots, two labs): R0 to R5 judged. The viewer's picture, B12, B13 and loss held in every row and every shaped flooder held its ceilings; the viewer's `desk-stream` CPU failed its 1-point rule in R1 (+1.1), R4 (+4.5, three repeats) and R5 (+1.75, unshaped neighbour); R5's negative control raised the guard's `throttle_bucket` as designed. The prod cost guard stopped each lab's host after two R4 floods within 15 minutes (a lab host without `HostKind=shared` is read as one desk); that, not SSH, lost the first lab's second R4 repeat. ## Unreleased - Apps that come with the desk: deskd hosts first-party apps from its own image (S-150, the Bible's desk side; `sub/bible-deskd`, in no release)
- **Added:** first-party apps bundled in deskd's image, today the Bible, can be added from the office's Apps sheet on every desk now, before the app helper, the Platform's catalog or review keys exist. They're added and removed like any app. Removing one keeps what the member made in it, and adding it again brings that back.
- **Added:** the member's data in a bundled app goes with their data export. The app's cache and deskd's record of it don't.
- **Added (Guild Desks):** the Founder adds apps for the guild, and each member uses them with their own data. A member who leaves the guild loses their data in every app, on the same safeguards as their agent conversations: deskd sends apps a new reserved call, `app.members.retain` (`appApi` 1.x), only on a roster it trusts, and never when it can't be sure of the key members' data is kept under. That key is kept beside the app's data, so a lost record changes nothing for members.
- **Changed (wire, additive):** `apps.catalog.get`, `apps.list` and `apps.view.get` carry `bundled`. A bundled app's view has no signatures: the Desk app checks it by its files' hashes alone (`services/deskd/docs/API.md` §19, "Bundled apps"). `GET /health`'s `apps` counts bundled apps and says how many the image offers.
- Status: `docs/sub-status/bible-deskd.md`.

The ring in the corner steps aside meanwhile, and comes back when the Browser stops covering it. Paying members see no change.

## Unreleased - Security's Lows before the next deskd (`sub/sec-lows-1111`; deskd and the self-hosted installer, in no release)
- **Fixed (Browser):** a desk whose owner becomes an adult (or whose band wasn't known) gets the adult Browser, password manager extensions included, without waiting for the Browser's next start: it restarts by itself the first moment nobody is using it. A teen's lock still comes at once, and a restart the band owes is no longer lost if deskd restarts first. Tabs, cookies and sign-ins carry over.
- **Fixed (simplified view):** a page's view kept while a long page redraws its rows is dropped as soon as the site moves to another page in the same tab (a web app's route, or `#` link), and after 5 seconds at most, so the view never shows the previous page's content.
- **Fixed (Agent):** the Agent can no longer type into, empty or Backspace in a box the page made read-only, or a field in a disabled group.
- **Fixed (self-hosted):** `ma-selfhost status` now says, beside a live pairing code, that whoever's office the desk joins gets its Browser, signed in, as `pair` does.
- Status: `docs/sub-status/sec-lows-1111.md`.

## Unreleased - Site sign-ins on desks, the office side (#11, S-142; `sub/signins-11`; Desk app, merged to `main` Nov 11 after Security's read; in no release)
- **Added:** a key button in the Browser bar, for adults on a desk whose deskd serves sign-ins (API.md section 10). With a password manager on the desk it's that manager ("Bitwarden, password manager") and opens its own page on the desk, and pressing it again closes it; with none it's "Passwords" and opens Passwords and sign-ins. `web/src/browser/extensions/`.
- **Added:** an Extensions menu ("Extensions on this desk") once a manager is installed: its row opens it, its pin puts it on the key, "Add a password manager" and "Passwords and sign-ins". Under 720 px of bar these rows are in More, above Picture quality; on a phone on its side under 700 px the key itself folds into More too. The first manager installed is pinned, per desk (layout pref `extPins`).
- **Added:** Passwords and sign-ins, a panel over the picture's right side (full width on a phone): a password manager from the desk's own list (Bitwarden, 1Password, Proton Pass), added only after "Add <Name> to this desk?" in place, which opens its Web Store page on the desk for the member's own Add; one passwords CSV from another browser, read on this device and sent only over the desk link's encrypted `bulk` channel into deskd's memory (`browser.import.begin`, the bytes, `browser.import.finish`), never to our servers or kept in the office; Passkeys; where passwords are kept, with "An honest limit"; and Stop (delete the saved passwords) once deskd serves a count.
- **Changed:** each CHUNK frame the office sends on `bulk` is zeroed once `send` has taken its copy (`web/src/desk/rtc/peer.ts`).
- **Kill switch:** client-config `siteSignIns: { enabled: false }`. Teens, an older deskd and a desk that refuses see the bar as before.
- **Not yet:** removing a manager (no `browser.extension.remove`), Stop on a real desk (no `browser.passwords.count` or `clear`), and anything on a real desk until the image's allowlist policy and a deskd release with sign-ins.

## Host image 0.1.11.4 - A new free desk on a shared host takes keys right away, and a new host's first desk is ready sooner (`maint/host-0.1.11`; baked, staging's image)
- **Fixed (Browser, shared hosts, ISS-96):** on a new free desk the Browser no longer ignores keys and clicks for about a minute after it first opens; Chromium's histogram file is on a small memory-backed folder, so its first-run writes never wait behind the desk's disk limit.
- **Faster (a new shared host):** the host reads its desk store once at its first boot, so its first desk's preparation takes about 12 to 32 s instead of 3 to 8 minutes.

## Host image 0.1.11.3 - A teen's desk on a shared host gets the Browser's teen lock (`sub/host-0.1.11.3`, `maint/host-0.1.11`; baked, nobody's default yet)
- **Fixed (Browser, shared hosts):** a teen's desk on a shared host, or one whose band isn't known yet, gets the Browser's teen lock (SafeSearch, YouTube's restricted mode, the family DNS, no extensions) before every start of the Browser, a crash restart included; if the lock can't be put in place the Browser doesn't start, and tries again by itself. Before, no shared host put it in place. Status: `docs/sub-status/host-0.1.11.3.md`.

## Host image 0.1.15 - Desks on a shared host get the Browser's links out and its teen lock and password-manager allowlist (`sub/host-parity-035`, `main`; baked, nobody's default yet)
- **Fixed (Browser, shared hosts):** a page's call, text, meeting, calendar and email links reach the desk's own service on a shared host too, as on a desk of its own; before, they did nothing there.
- **Fixed (Browser, shared hosts):** a teen's desk on a shared host gets the Browser's teen lock, and an adult's the password-manager allowlist, before every start of the Browser; if the lock can't be put in place the Browser doesn't start, and tries again by itself. Before, neither was put in place on a shared host.
- Status: `docs/sub-status/host-parity-035.md` and `docs/sub-status/host-0.1.15.md` (the bake, its verify runs and lab).

## Unreleased - Open tiles: an app enlarged on its desk is the app itself, right there (ADR 0053; `sub/open-tiles`; the Desk app, not released)
- **Added:** make an app's tile big enough (6 by 5 cells, the Browser 7 by 5) and it becomes the app, live on the desk: write in the Notebook, browse, open folders in Files, talk to your agents, use Commons on a Guild Desk, without opening a window. Its slim head moves it, its corners and edges resize it, and "Open in its window" opens its window. Several can be open side by side, up to four at once on the desk you're looking at.
- **Added:** while an app's window is open, its enlarged tile says "Open in its window"; close the window and the app is back in its tile, showing what it showed.
- **Changed:** a narrow Notebook (a tile, or a window under 640 px) puts its notebooks and pages behind a button in its head, so the page has the width. A narrow Files keeps its buttons on one row. In Zen the Notebook is slate, like the rest of the office.
- Zoomed out until a tile is under 480 by 320 on screen, or on another desk, it's the tile as before. Phones keep their grid. Keys and Esc inside the app are the app's; Esc it doesn't use, or Shift+Esc, goes back to the tile.
- Kill switch: client-config `openTiles: { enabled: false }`. Status: `docs/sub-status/open-tiles.md`.

## Unreleased - Self-hosted desks: the home-network rule holds even if its helper stops, and nothing traces the pairing prompt (Security's follow-ups to EXP-9 and the `ptrace` note; `sub/selfhost-followups`, `docs/sub-status/selfhost-exp.md` "Nov 11 follow-up")
- **Changed:** if the part that keeps the desk's list of your home networks ever stops, the desk's Browser uses IPv4 alone until it's back (within 3 minutes, and nothing you'd notice: sites answer over IPv4), so a page on the desk can't reach a home device the list hasn't caught up with.
- **Security:** while `sudo ma-selfhost pair --wait` asks whether the office shown is yours, the desk's identity service, which runs as the same user, can no longer trace it.

## Unreleased - The desk image's side of four launch features (desk image 0.3.5, `sub/image-035-asks`; not baked yet)
- **Added (Agent):** a desk's Agent can reach OpenRouter, so the free models the office already offers answer instead of failing as unavailable.
- **Added (Browser):** a page's call, text, Zoom, Teams, FaceTime, calendar and email links are handed to the device the member is using, instead of doing nothing on the desk (the office's offer to open them comes with the deskd release that has links out).
- **Added (Browser):** an adult's desk can install Bitwarden, 1Password or Proton Pass from the Chrome Web Store; no other extension installs, on any desk. A teen's desk gets none. An extension a member installed before is turned off, not removed.
- **Fixed (simplified view):** the desk's Browser no longer asks sites for JPEG XL pictures, which the simplified view can't show and which some sites (the Guardian's front page) sent for every picture.
- Status: `docs/sub-status/image-035-asks.md`.

## Unreleased - Self-hosted desks: the installer has its own signing key (`sub/selfhost-release-key`, `docs/sub-status/selfhost-release-key.md`)
- **Changed:** installers and bundles for a desk on your own computer are signed with a key used for nothing else, the self-hosted release key (sha256 `a334decc5565bfef53d704e30f11041b5e71b499d8677c779ec8a3c2f7024633`), no longer with the key desks take update batches from. The installer prints that sha256 when it starts; the `/self-hosted` page and the guide's "Checking the installer yourself" show it to compare. A desk keeps the key, and an update signed with any other is refused. Internal test desks installed from a bundle signed the old way are refused an update and are installed fresh; no member has one.

## Unreleased - Self-hosted desks: signed installers, the home network over IPv6, and root kept out of the services' folders (EXP-7, EXP-9, EXP-10; `sub/selfhost-exp`, `docs/sub-status/selfhost-exp.md`)
- **Changed:** the installer installs only a bundle, and installer files, signed with Minimalist Alliance's release key, and stops before changing anything otherwise. An update takes only the key the desk already has; `sudo ma-selfhost verify <folder>` checks a new folder first. The guide says how to check a first install by hand ("Checking the installer yourself"). Bundles from before this aren't signed, so this installer refuses them.
- **Fixed:** by default the desk's Browser couldn't reach your home network's private addresses, but could reach its devices' global IPv6 addresses (and a home network on public IPv4). It can't now, and the desk keeps up as your network changes.
- **Fixed:** steps that run as root on the desk no longer act on names inside a service's own folders, where a compromised service could have pointed them elsewhere. Cloud desks get the two recipe changes (`desk-datadirs`, `desk-browser-close`) at their next image.

## Unreleased - Desks on a shared host start after many of its slots are made ready at once (host image 0.1.11.1; `maint/host-0.1.11`, `sub/prepare-space`; `docs/sub-status/host-0.1.11.1.md`)
- **Fixed:** when Platform made several of a shared host's slots ready again at once, the host's disk for desk copies could fill, and desks placed on that host afterwards failed to start ("No space left on device"). A host now makes one slot ready at a time, removes the slot's old copy first, turns a request it has no room for away cleanly, and leaves nothing behind when one fails. Nothing a desk keeps (its files, its browser session) is on that disk.

## Unreleased - No square tap highlight on phones (`sub/tap-highlight`; Desk app, in no release)
- **Fixed:** pressing a key or button on a phone no longer shows the browser's grey square behind its round pressed state. Icon buttons, menu items, buttons, the Browser's tabs and More menu now show their own pressed tint. CSS only.
## Unreleased - Self-hosted desks: the pairing code kept private, and a warning before the Browser's sign-ins join an office (Security's EXP-8, S-85; `sub/exp8-desk`; with the next installer, no bundle change)
- **Fixed:** only `ma-selfhost`'s own user and root can read a self-hosted desk's live pairing code (`/run/ma-selfhost/status.json` is `0640` in a `0750` directory, and never wider while it's written), so another account on the computer can't pair the desk into its own office; and once the desk has run, `ma-selfhost pair` and `forget` (so the installer) first say that whoever's office the desk joins gets its Browser, signed in, and ask y/N, refusing with no terminal unless given `--yes`. A desk gets both when its installer is run again.

## Unreleased - Paste in the phone's key row (#12, S-143; `sub/touch-paste`; Desk app, in no release)
- **Added:** Paste, a clipboard key beside Hide the keyboard in the typing row, puts what you copied on your phone into the desk's page. It reads your phone's clipboard only when you tap it, once per tap, and keeps nothing. If your browser says no it says "Your browser didn't allow that paste."; an empty clipboard says "There's nothing to paste." While typing, the Browser's notices now show as a small pill above the key row, since the bar steps aside. `web/src/browser/touchPaste.ts`, `TouchRow.tsx`.

## Unreleased - The setup circle's What's new glyph is a plain dot (`sub/setup-dot`; the Desk app, not released)
- **Changed:** after setup, the circle's What's new glyph is a plain filled dot in the same grey as the other glyphs, not a star (the Designer's flag).

## Unreleased - A phone's first visit never starts a desk you didn't pick (`sub/phone-arrival-wake`; the Desk app, not released)
- **Fixed:** on a phone with nothing remembered, the office landed on the first desk in reading order and started it, even when another desk was already running. It now lands on the first desk that is running, and starts nothing. With none running it lands on the first desk that can open, asleep, and a tap on the desk's apps starts it. A desk you chose before still starts when you arrive on it, as every screen does.

## Unreleased - Browser and Agent windows at 400 % zoom, and an address kept while the Browser starts (`sub/browser-followups`; the Desk app, not released)
- **Fixed:** at 320 and 256 CSS px (the screen at 400 % zoom) the Browser's tab row keeps New tab in view at its end, tabs shrink before the strip scrolls, the address bar's buttons fall to a second row under 360 px, the starting and waking panel's four-step bar fits, and the Agent's provider switch and finished-tool chips wrap inside the window.
- **Fixed:** an address typed in the Browser while it is still starting stays in the box when you click away, instead of being replaced by the page's address. Press Enter and it opens when the Browser is up, as before.

## Unreleased - Touch control on phones, part 2: the keyboard and the phone view (#12, S-143; `sub/touch-keyboard`; Desk app, in no release; the phone view needs deskd bfaa1ee2 or later)
- **Added:** a phone types into the desk's page. Keyboard, in the row under the picture, raises the phone's own keyboard; what's typed goes as the desk needs it, so autocorrect, predictions, swipe typing, dictation and emoji work, and Backspace and Enter do. While typing, the row becomes the keys a phone lacks (Esc, Tab, Ctrl, the arrows, Alt, Menu, Home, End, PgUp, PgDn, Del) and Hide the keyboard; Ctrl and Alt stay on for the next key, or locked with a second tap. The page gets the screen while typing, and a small desk picture zooms to the place last tapped. Before the desk says what field has focus, the phone's keyboard corrects and learns nothing. `web/src/browser/softKeys.ts`, `softKeyboard.ts`, `TouchRow.tsx`.
- **Added (with deskd's phone view):** on a phone the desk lays its pages out for the phone and the picture shows just that, so a finger is a finger to the page: a tap taps, a drag scrolls with the page's own momentum, a hold opens the page's menu. A field taking focus raises the keyboard by itself (not on iOS, where Keyboard is the way in). Desktop site, in the row, keeps the desk's own layout. A computer watching meanwhile says "Your phone has this page. Click it to bring it back here." `web/src/browser/phoneView.ts`; `desk.attention.set` carries the phone's size.
- **Changed (simplified view):** on a phone, choosing the real page stops the simplified view watching that page, so the desk can lay it out for the phone.
- **Not yet:** Paste from the phone's clipboard (waits for Security), the selection chip, "Type here", a portrait picture (the System Engineer's).

## Unreleased - Reflow at 400 % and three accessibility leftovers (`sub/a11y-reflow`; the Desk app, not released)
- **Fixed:** a re-fit of the desk you are on (a resize, or the desk list answering) no longer raises it, so on a phone the saved stacking of your desks stays as it was (`sub/desks-phone-flake`).
- **Fixed:** at 400 % zoom on a 1024 px screen (a 256 px viewport) the account menu and the bell's panel ran off the left edge and Maia's panel header spilled out; they fit now, and her sheet is as tall as the screen when the screen is short. No horizontal scroll at 320 or 256 px in the bars, menus, panels, settings, notices and the setup circle.
- **Fixed:** Space presses See membership (and Try it) on the setup panel and Maia's trial note, as it does on the notice card.
- **Fixed:** the bandwidth circle showed two rings in Zen and Dark; one ring now, as in Light.
- No new words. Status: `docs/sub-status/a11y-desk.md`.

## Unreleased - Free models through OpenRouter in the Agent's key screen (S-138, #6; `sub/agent-free-key`)
- **Added (Agent):** "Try free models", first on the key screen, then "or" and "Add your own key" (the Product Designer's spec, taste call A). **Connect OpenRouter** runs OpenRouter's PKCE sign-in in the member's own browser: the office tab makes the verifier, opens openrouter.ai in a window of its own, trades the code its new `/connect/openrouter` page relays for the member's own key at OpenRouter, and hands the key to the desk with `agent.key.add`, like a pasted one. No key, code or verifier reaches Minimalist Alliance, a log or storage beyond the tab's own pending Connect. The privacy line is always in view in the card. Its states: waiting, a blocked window (with a link), checking, and a line for every way it can fail.
- **Added (Agent):** after it's set up, the "OR" badge, the empty thread's one note, "free" in a reply's foot, the usage card's line, Keys' "· Free" and "· Chats only" models with their tags and short privacy line, the key's "Free models only" line and **Try free models** under "Add a key". Today's free requests used is a warn banner with **Add a key**, not a failure; OpenRouter's busy, slow and privacy-settings failures have their own words and **Pick another model**.
- **Changed:** the setup circle's Agent line: "Try free models with no card, or use a key of your own. Either way, the key stays on your desk." The paste form's help line for OpenRouter.
- **Not offered** to a teen (who has no Agent), on a Guild Desk (paste only), or by a desk whose `desk-agent` doesn't list OpenRouter. A Connect finishes only once the office CSP allows `https://openrouter.ai` (ma-platform `sub/agent-free-key-csp`, contract 0.3.90 draft, not applied) and the desk image lists `openrouter.ai` in `agent-egress.conf` (System Engineer); until then the card says why and adds nothing.
- Tests: `web/agent/src/connect/openrouter.test.ts`, `model/agents.test.ts`; `tests/e2e/agent.free.shell.spec.ts` (16) against a stand-in for OpenRouter routed in the browser, never the real one. Status: `docs/sub-status/agent-free-key.md`.

## Unreleased - The ended Work desk and the Files over-drive line (`sub/billing-142b`; the Desk app, not released)
- **Added:** a Work desk a step down has stopped stays on the canvas with its tiles at 45%, and opening it shows a panel over the desk: "Your Work desk stopped on {date}", "It isn't part of {tier}. We keep a snapshot of it until {date}, and you can download everything on it until then.", with Download your data (Account settings, Your data) and Move back up (billing, a new tab). Where Platform keeps the desk with no snapshot date (`notInPlan`, contract 0.3.24), the body says "It's kept, with its files, tabs and sign-ins, until you move back up, and you can download everything on it." A lazy chunk (`chrome/EndedDeskPanel.tsx`).
- **Added:** Files' drive line, when a paid desk keeps more than its drive holds (a step down): "You have {used} in Files, more than {drive}. It all stays readable; to add more, make room or move back up." with See membership. A free desk's full line is unchanged.
- Nothing shows without its field (`notInPlan`, or a retained Work desk with `export` alone; Files' `capBytes` below what's used). First load 245.9 KB of 250. Status: `docs/sub-status/billing-142b.md`.

## Unreleased - The billing launch features, the office's part (`sub/billing-142`; the Desk app, not released)
- **Added:** the account menu's line for a card that expires before the next charge, "Card ending 4242 expires 11/26. Update card", from `/v1/me` `membership.cardExpiresSoon` (ma-platform contract 0.3.86), and a failed payment's "Your last payment didn't go through. Pay now" ahead of it once Platform sends `membership.billingNote`.

- Nothing shows without those fields. Nothing added to the first load: 245.8 KB of 250, the same as main. Status: `docs/sub-status/billing-142.md`.

## Unreleased - Touch control on phones, part 1: gestures (#12, S-143; `sub/touch-gestures`; Desk app, in no release)
- **Added:** a finger on the Browser's picture controls the page, on the wire that exists (no `desk-rtc` or `deskd` change), to the Product Designer's spec (f). A tap clicks as the finger lifts; a double or triple tap is a double or triple click; a drag scrolls the page, which follows the finger, keeping to up and down (or sideways) once it starts that way, and a quick one carries on a little; a long press then lift is the right button, for the page's menu; a long press then drag selects. Two fingers zoom and move the picture on the member's screen only, filling the Browser's area; nothing of the zoom reaches the desk. A dot shows where the finger landed, and a ring fills while it holds. `web/src/browser/touchInput.ts`, `touchFeedback.ts`.
- **Added (on a touch screen):** a row under the picture (a column at its right on a phone on its side) with Zoom out, Zoom in and, while zoomed, Fit the page. `web/src/browser/TouchRow.tsx`.
- **Changed:** "Touch control isn't here yet. You can watch." is gone. A member's first touch on a picture in this browser shows a card, "Tap to click, drag to scroll, pinch to zoom." / "Hold for the page's menu, or hold and drag to select.", with Got it; it goes on Got it or after their third gesture, and doesn't come back. Screen readers hear it once, and the picture now says it's a picture a screen reader can't read, pointing to Simplified.
- **Changed (carousel on a phone):** a sideways swipe that starts within 16 px of the screen's left or right edge moves the carousel even over the Browser's picture (`fromScreenEdge` in `carousel/model.ts`, used in `CarouselLayer.tsx`'s `touchstart`); up and down from there, and anywhere else on the picture, the page scrolls. The thumb row is unchanged.
- **Not yet:** part 2, typing from a phone (the Keyboard button, the key row, the selection chip; Paste from the phone's clipboard waits for Security), and phone-shaped pages with Desktop site (wait for the Desk and System Engineers).

## Unreleased - Links in and out (#9, owner-approved launch feature, S-141; `sub/links-9`; needs the next Desk app release)
- **Added:** Open on this device, a button at the right end of the Browser's address (↗): it opens the tab's page in the member's own browser. The first time for the account a card in the picture says "Opened on this device. Your desk's sign-ins stay on your desk, so you may need to sign in there."; after that the bar's note says "Opened on this device" for 3 s. Only for http and https pages, and not while a new address is typed.
- **Added:** the desk hands over a link: when a page on the desk opens an email, call, text, Zoom, Teams or calendar link, a card at the top of the picture names the kind in our own words ("This page wants to start an email.") with the address as plain text, "Open on this device" and "Not now". Nothing opens without the member's press; after it, "If nothing opened, this device may not have an app for it." with "Copy the address". It reads deskd's `browser.links` (`linkOut`, built, in no release yet, and the image's side isn't baked), so until both ship there's never a card.
- **Added:** Send to my desk: a link sent to `office…/open#u=<url>` (an iOS Shortcut or a bookmarklet), `/open?u=&t=&n=` (a share target, once the office's manifest has one) or pasted on the office (Ctrl+V or ⌘V with focus on the office itself) shows a sheet, "Open this link on a desk", naming the link and the member's desks with a Browser. It opens in a new tab on the chosen desk without opening the Browser window there, waking it if asleep, and a line in the notices stack says so, with Show me; or that it couldn't, with Try again. Signed out, the sign-in card says "Sign in to open this link" and the link waits in that tab only. Only http and https; anything else gets "There's no web link here".
- **Added:** "Get it on this device" on a finished download, once deskd says the download's own address (`BrowserDownload.url`, asked of the Desk Engineer).
- **Changed:** the protected-video note reads "This video is protected, so it can't play on a desk." (the Product Designer, Nov 11), and its card is the picture's shared card (`browser/cards.css`).
- **Changed:** under 720 px of Browser bar, Picture quality folds into a More menu at the bar's end, which turns to the quality menu's own pages.
- **Changed:** the Keyboard shortcuts list adds "Ctrl+V: Open a copied link on a desk" (⌘V on a Mac).
- client-config `browserLinks: { enabled: false }` is the kill switch for the hand-off, `/open`, the paste and the download's button. The sheet is its own chunk; initial JS 245.7 KB of 250, about 1.2 KB of it this feature's.

## Unreleased - The simplified view never touches a password manager's frames (`sub/simple-view-quality`; deskd, in no release; the sign-ins work's finding)
- **Security (`deskd`):** a frame that isn't a web page's, such as a password manager's in-page menu (`chrome-extension:`), is never armed, walked or read by the simplified view, nor through it by the Agent: deskd puts nothing in a frame until it commits to an `http` or `https` page, lets go of one that commits to anything else, checks each frame's origin again whenever it would run anything in it, and its own half in such a document refuses every op. A page's actions refuse while such a frame is on it, as for any frame that can't be guarded. `/health` counts these frames, never which (`services/deskd/docs/API.md` §14).

## Unreleased - The simplified view never touches a password manager's frames (`sub/simple-view-quality`; deskd, in no release; the sign-ins work's finding)
- **Security (`deskd`):** a frame that isn't a web page's, such as a password manager's in-page menu (`chrome-extension:`), is never armed, walked or read by the simplified view, nor through it by the Agent: deskd puts nothing in a frame until it commits to an `http` or `https` page, lets go of one that commits to anything else, checks each frame's origin again whenever it would run anything in it, and its own half in such a document refuses every op. A page's actions refuse while such a frame is on it, as for any frame that can't be guarded. `/health` counts these frames, never which (`services/deskd/docs/API.md` §14).

## Unreleased - Desks on shared hosts take Debian's and Chromium's security fixes (ADR 0077's member-host half, `sub/u77-host`, `docs/sub-status/u77-host.md`; the host image after 0.1.10, not baked)
- **Added:** a shared host fetches each signed template generation once, checks and seals it, and each desk moves to it at its own stop, or while idle for a desk that never stops (about 10 s with nobody on it). Nothing changes under a running desk, and the browser session (cookies, tabs, saved passwords) is kept across every move. A desk whose profile ran a newer Chromium never moves back to an older one, a rollback included.
- **Added:** the host's own packages take Debian's security archive daily with nothing restarted, and the host reboots only on Platform's order once every desk is idle; each desk closes as a stop closes it and comes back with its session (about 30 s in the lab).
- Needs Platform's PU-1 and PU-2 (the report's update fields, `template_target`, `template_promote`, `template_rollback`, `reboot_host`) and the signing key (`alias/ma-host-template`) before a host takes any generation.

## Unreleased - ADR 0036 section 6 tests 1, 2 and 4 on a desk of its own (TD-223; `sub/guild-td223-test1`; test tooling and results only)
- **Added:** `image/lab/throttle/real/`, which runs section 6 through the real stack on `Purpose=browser-benchmark` boxes from the current desk image (`real.sh`, `rows.sh`, `rtctap.py` for `rtpDropped`, `t1judge.py` with 6 unit tests, `pause.mjs`), and the evidence in `image/evidence/throttle-real-2026-11-11/`. Nothing in the image, a release or a service changes.
- **Results** (ADR 0036, the addendum of 2026-11-09's result of 2026-11-11): on `ma-desk-0.3.3` at 1080p, test 1 passes with the shaper idle and with the bucket empty. With `kiosk` saturating 1:20 it **fails its CPU rule**, at `desk-stream` +2.2 and +2.4 points against 1; the picture, B12, B13 and loss are unchanged. Tests 2 and 4 pass. Test 1 on a shared host can't run until a per-desk shaper exists on the veth (System Engineer).

## Unreleased - The Agent's tools: Files, Browser and Notebook, each asking first (S-14, `sub/agent-tools`; desk-agent, deskd and desk-notebook, in no release)
- **Added:** an agent can work with the member's Files (list, search, read, save, make folders, move, move to the trash), browse in a browser of its own that's signed in to nothing (open, read, type, go back, close, and use one of the member's signed-in sites for one task only with their yes), and read and edit the Notebook (search, read as Markdown, create, append, replace a passage, move to the trash), plus a checklist on its reply (`plan_update`), a follow-up it asks to set (`follow_up`) and deleting from its own workspace. Every change to the member's things, every send and every use of their session asks the member first; deskd checks the yes again before it runs. Nothing is ever deleted for good, and payment fields are never typed. A guild member's own agent gets the Notebook only. `services/deskd/docs/API.md` §18, `services/desk-agent/README.md` "Tools".
- **Added:** an agent can click and press keys on a page (`browser_click`, `browser_press`), done by deskd's own script in its own world on the element the member's card showed, so they work on the desk's Chromium where real input doesn't reach the Agent's tabs. Submitting a form asks as a send; anything that looks like paying, or any click on a page with a payment provider's frame, asks as a spend; in the member's signed-in site every click asks, a link as egress, and a link off that site is refused. Only a task the member started may ask for their signed-in site. Asking under the Rule of Two is now for browse actions only: reading, listing tabs and closing one never ask. A frame of another site no longer gets past the block on local addresses. `sub/agent-click-press`, `services/deskd/docs/API.md` §14 and §18.
- **For releases:** `desk-notebook` before `deskd`; `deskd` and `desk-agent` in either order, as desk-agent sends no tool call before deskd's hello and an older desk-agent answers the hello `not_found`.
- **Fixed (`deskd`):** an agent's `files_write` and a Files upload's begin could both see the same last bytes free (a free desk's cap, or the floor) and both take them, as the write didn't share the upload's lock. It does now, from its check of the room until its file is in place and counted (`services/deskd/docs/files.md` §5.1).

## Unreleased - Touch control on phones, deskd's part (`sub/touch-control`; deskd, not released; needs the Desk app's half)
- **Added:** while the session that looked at the Browser last is a phone's (`desk.attention.set` with `device: {kind: "phone", width, height}`), deskd lays out every tab as that phone would (mobile viewport at its CSS size, scaled to fit the screen, and touch emulation over CDP), so the X pointer desk-rtc moves is a finger to the page: a tap taps, a drag scrolls, a hold is a long press. A desktop's first input after a second's rest, the phone leaving, or the simplified view in use gives the desk its own view back. Flips wait 0.8 s, and 4 s between a phone's view and the desk's. No reload, no user agent change, nothing in the profile (ADR 0035); the tab on screen keeps its place. desk-rtc is unchanged.
- **Added:** topic `browser.view` (which view, the phone's region of the picture, and the field focused in the tab on screen, so a phone can raise its keyboard), `browser.field.at` (what a tap would focus, for iOS) and `browser.pinch`. API.md section 10, "On a phone".
- Tests: `BrowserViewTest` (19, fake CDP: a phone joining and leaving, a desktop taking over, debouncing, the simplified view, a pinch, the focus event, a tap's question) and `BrowserViewChromiumIT` (7, real Chromium with local pages: what a page sees, a press as a finger, the binding with no `Runtime.enable`, a pinch in both views, the place kept both ways). Real X input checked once by hand on Xvfb.

## Unreleased - Past the data allowance, picture quality says it's held, and Low stays Low (`sub/s29-capped-stream`, S-29; `desk-rtc` 0.5.1 and `desk-stream` 0.3.5 for the next bake; on the owner's desk since 2026-11-11 after the test desk's `e2e/` passed, on no other member desk)
- **Fixed:** past 100 GB a month the Browser's picture menu said the member's own level (Max, say) while the desk sent the capped picture. The desk now answers "held", and the menu already says "This desk is held at Medium for now." (Low past 500 GB.)
- **Fixed:** a member on Low past 100 GB got the capped picture, sharper and about 60% more data than Low. Low now stays Low there; Medium, High and Max get the capped picture.
- **Fixed:** two or three people watching one desk nonstop, with a big upload beside them, could have their video held to 1 Mbit for hours, or the desk stopped by the cost guard. The picture now softens earlier instead, and the 1 Mbit hold is only for a broken stream server. Frame rate is never traded either way.
- **Fixed (`desk-stream` 0.3.5):** when the stream server restarted with a member on Max, it could fail to start a few times first. That took the picture away for up to about 4 seconds. It now starts clean every time: 20 of 20 at Max on a real desk, against 6 of 10 before.

## Unreleased - Signing in that works on the desk (`sub/desk-sign-ins`; deskd, in no release; needs the image's policies and the Desk app's key button)
- **Added (`deskd`):** `browser.import {kind, path}` brings a browser's bookmarks (HTML) in from Files, and `browser.import.begin`, `finish` and `abort` its passwords (CSV), through Chromium's own Password Manager and bookmarks in hidden pages of Chromium's own, so only Chromium writes the profile and nothing already there is replaced. `browser.bookmarks.list` shows bookmarks a folder at a time, as a kiosk has no bookmarks bar. The key button's `browser.extensions.list`, `browser.extension.open` and `close` open an allowed password manager's own page (Bitwarden, 1Password, Proton Pass) in a tab deskd never attaches to, or its Web Store page. Not on a teen's desk. Chrome sync stays off: Google lets only Chrome use it (`services/deskd/docs/API.md` §10, "Signing in"; `docs/sub-status/desk-sign-ins.md`).
- **Security (`deskd`):** a passwords file never touches the desk's disks, so no snapshot of its volumes can keep it (before, it went through Files and was deleted after, which a snapshot taken in between didn't see). The Desk app sends it from the member's own browser straight into deskd's memory: `browser.import.begin {kind: "passwords", size}` (at most 2 MiB), the bytes on `bulk` as a Files upload sends them, then `browser.import.finish {uploadId}`. deskd zeroes its buffers after the import and on every other way out (abort, a bad frame, 60 s idle, the session's end, deskd's stop); deskd's container can't swap, and nothing is logged. `browser.import` with `kind: "passwords"` is now refused (`bad_request`, `reason: "use_upload"`), and a passwords file a member puts in Files is never read or deleted by deskd. The reply has no `deleted` field any more. Needs the Desk app's import screen to send the file this way.

## Unreleased - Ctrl+W without fear (#16, owner-approved launch feature; `sub/ctrlw-guard`; needs the next Desk app release)
- **Added:** closing or reloading the office asks first, with the browser's own "Leave site?" prompt, only while something would be lost: a Maia or Agent draft with text, an Agent settings form with changes, a Notebook page not yet on its desk, a Files transfer running, or the export running. Nothing asks for a desk being connected or a window being open, and a sign-out never asks. One registry, `web/src/shell/closeGuard.ts`, that every app reports into; the Notebook's and the export's own guards moved onto it.
- **Added:** in full screen on Chrome and Edge, the office takes Keyboard Lock (`web/src/shell/keyLock.ts`): Ctrl+W, T, N, Ctrl+Tab, Ctrl+PageUp/PageDown, Ctrl+L and Ctrl+R/F5 (Cmd on a Mac) no longer close or leave the office, and in the Browser's picture they go to the desk. Escape isn't locked, so one press still leaves full screen. Safari and Firefox are unchanged. The Keyboard shortcuts list says so on Chromium: "In full screen, Ctrl+W, T, N and Tab go to your desk."
- **Added:** the installed office (Platform's loader manifest): in its own window, Ctrl+W, T and N in the Browser's picture go to the desk, full screen or not. While Chrome or Edge offers to install it, the Keyboard shortcuts section ends with "Install the office as an app" and "In its own window, Ctrl+W and Ctrl+T go to your desk."
- The Agent app takes `onUnsaved` (`web/agent`). The Keyboard Lock chunk is 0.5 KB, loaded on the first full screen; initial JS 243.8 to 244.1 KB of 250.

## Unreleased - Self-hosted desks on image 0.3.3's software, and ready for signed updates (S-85, `sub/s85-selfhost-033`, `docs/sub-status/s85-selfhost-033.md`; staging only)
- **Changed:** a self-hosted desk installed or updated with this installer and bundle runs the same software as a cloud desk on image 0.3.3: `desk-rtc` 0.5.0, `desk-stream` 0.3.3, `deskd` `2026.11.10-ff58751`, `desk-notebook` `2026.11.10-c472928`, `desk-agent-filter` 0.1.1, and, new on these desks, **the Agent** (`desk-agent` `2026.11.10-7e809cb`). Its services restart with the cloud desks' back-off and kill order, and "Download your data" has its helper.
- **Changed:** Debian's updates on a self-hosted desk now come only from Debian's security archive and only while nobody is using the desk (or after 3 days with no quiet moment), as on a cloud desk. Stable point releases no longer install by themselves, and nothing restarts the computer: a new kernel waits for the member's next restart.
- **Added (desk side only):** a self-hosted desk checks and applies signed update batches built for its processor (`"arch": "amd64"`), while idle, and refuses a batch signed with any other key, built for another machine, or naming an image. The platform doesn't send batches to self-hosted desks yet, so updating is still `install.sh` again with a newer bundle.
- **Fixed:** a new self-hosted desk no longer copies a Guild Desk's files, whose tmpfiles line names a user these machines don't have, and `/etc/ma-release` names the bundle's commit instead of `selfhost-unknown` when the installer was copied without its `.git`.

## Unreleased - Links out: a desk page's mailto, tel and meeting links reach the member's own device (Links in and out; `sub/links-out`; in no release, and the image's side isn't baked)
- **Added (`deskd`):** the topic `browser.links`, whose `linkOut {url, scheme, displayText, reason, at, tabId?}` tells owner sessions with video and input of a link Chromium handed the desk's OS, so the office can offer to open it on the member's device (`services/deskd/docs/API.md` §10, "Links out"). Only `mailto`, `tel`, `sms`, `zoommtg`, `msteams`, `facetime`, `webcal`, `http` and `https` go, cleaned to printable ASCII, at most 3 in 10 s and never the same URL twice in 5 s; everything else is dropped and counted in `/health`. The links come from `services/deskd/desk-tools/xdg-open`, a stand-in for `xdg-open` and `xdg-email` that Chromium runs as `kiosk`, over `/run/desk-links/links.sock` (`docs/interfaces/desk-runtime.md` §8.11), which the image has yet to install; until then SUB gets `not_found`. "Send to my desk" needs nothing new: `browser.tab.open {url}` takes only `http` and `https`.

## Unreleased - Cards C and D dim the stopped desks behind them (S-82, the Product Designer's call for release 13; `sub/s82-dim`)
- **Changed:** while card C (payment-suspended) or D (ended by the member, desks kept) shows over the desks, the desks are drawn at 35 percent (tiles, glyphs and the etched name) as one group, and carry no counts: no app badges, no hidden-apps count, none announced to screen readers. The card stays centred where it is. Active members, E, F, A and B are unchanged. `canvas/stoppedDim.ts` decides it from `noDeskNotice`; `WorldStage` sets the opacity once on the `desks` group.

## Unreleased - Shared hosts slow a desk's outbound before the cost guard stops it (TD-223, `sub/td223-shaper`; for host image 0.1.11, not baked)
- **Added:** on a shared host each desk gets ADR 0036's outbound shaper, as a desk of its own has had since image 0.2.4: its video, its own background traffic and everything else (the Browser's uploads) each in a class of its own at the class's ceiling (60, 2 and 100 Mbit/s for a paid desk, half for a free one), and the same budgets: past 30 GB of uploads the Browser's slow to 0.75 Mbit/s until the bucket refills, and past 100 GB this month the picture softens, at the same frame rate. Nothing changes for a desk under its limits; the stream's class is never slowed by the bucket. The cost guard's stop stays, now as the last resort.
- **For operators:** `desk-host shaper <k> show|off|on`, the report's `shaper` per running slot, `desk-host-check shaper`, `/etc/desk-host/egress.conf`. `image/host/README.md`, "The outbound shaper".

## Unreleased - The Browser's note when protected playback fails (TD-225; `sub/td225-drm-note`; not shipped)
- **Added:** when the desk's Chromium refuses protected playback (EME or Widevine), the Browser window shows a small calm note over the tab where it happened: "This video is protected, and protected playback doesn't work on desks yet. Play it on your own device." with an "Open on this device" link (the page's address in a new tab of the member's own browser, `browser/openOnDevice.ts`) and a Dismiss button. A dismissed site gets no note again until the page is reloaded. No site is blocked and no service is said to work.
- **Signal:** the desk's `protectedMedia {at, keySystem}` on a tab of `browser.tabs` (deskd da3738de, `API.md` "A protected video that can't play"), set once per page and cleared by a new document. Read only by `browser/protectedPlayback.ts`. A desk before it never has the field, so never shows a note.
- Tests: `protectedPlayback.test.ts` (adapter, once-per-site rule, link helper), `browser.protected.shell.spec.ts` (words, link, tab, dismissal, a desk without the event, Light, Zen, Dark shots).

## Unreleased - Tooling: token parity check and perf-pr (TD-81, TD-73; `sub/td81-td73`; no app change in a release)
- **Added:** `web/scripts/check-token-parity.mjs` (desk against landing tokens, per theme, shared roles; first run found 34 differences, 30 of them Zen, which the landing hasn't taken ADR 0093 for) and `web/scripts/perf-pr.mjs` with `web/perf-baseline.json` (pan and zoom fps, window open time, bundle size). `?perf=1` on the dev server shows an fps and long-task readout; a build drops it. How to run both: `web/CLAUDE.md`.

## Unreleased - deskd's heartbeat names its own version and /health answer, for shared hosts' releases (ADR 0078 DE-1, DE-2; `sub/slot-files`; deskd, in no release)
- **Added (`deskd`):** every heartbeat carries `deskd: {version, status}`, this deskd's version and `GET /health`'s own `ok` or `failing`, which the Platform's promote of a shared host's canary batch reads; a failing one also makes `health.status` failing (`services/deskd/docs/API.md` §20, `docs/interfaces/desk-runtime.md` §6). DE-1 holds as it is: deskd doesn't relay batches yet, so a desk with no `batch.sock` (a shared host's slot) makes no offer, logs no batch error and sends no `update`, now pinned by a test.
- **Found (free walk H5, host images up to 0.1.10):** Files on a shared host's desk answers `unavailable` `{reason: "tree"}` ("Your desk couldn't open that just now") because the slot's sandbox gives deskd only `/data/desk`, not the volume's `/data/files`. The fix is the host image's (System Engineer); deskd is unchanged (API.md §16).

## Unreleased - OpenRouter for a $0 first Agent run (`sub/openrouter`; desk-agent, needs its next release, the image's name filter listing `openrouter.ai`, and the Agent app's Connect button)
- **Added:** desk-agent's fourth provider, OpenRouter (`openrouter`, keys `sk-or-…`, Chat Completions streamed). `agent.key.add` takes `provider: "openrouter"`, checked on OpenRouter's key endpoint; `agent.key.models` lists its free models only, the best first, each with `free` and `tools`. Free models cost a real $0, so no dollar limit refuses them (token limits still bind), and a cost OpenRouter reports is charged. A model without tools still chats and is told it has none. Today's free limit ends a reply with `provider_error` and `errorDetail: "free_limit"`; privacy settings that allow no endpoint, `data_policy`. Paid OpenRouter models aren't listed yet. desk-agent `README.md` "OpenRouter", deskd `API.md` §11. Tests: `OpenRouterProviderTest`, `OpenRouterGatewayTest`, `OpenRouterSocketTest`, `PricingTest`, all on a local stand-in.

## Unreleased - A kill switch for the protected-media note (TD-225; `deskd`, in no release)
- `/data/desk/protected-media-switch` with the word `off` stops `deskd` putting its script in member tabs and removes it from open ones, ignores its binding, and drops `protectedMedia` from `browser.tab.list` and `browser.tabs`. No file means on. Read while `deskd` runs, so it needs no release. `services/deskd/docs/API.md` section 10.

## Unreleased - Cards C and D show over a stopped member's desks (S-82; `sub/s82-cards-over-desks`; Desk app, in no release)
- **Changed:** a suspended (payment) or ended (their own choice) member still has desks, stopped. They now see card C ("Your desks are paused") or D ("Your membership has ended") centred over the canvas, the desks visible behind it, with the same words, links and export as on the empty office. No new scrim and no dismiss. E, F, A, B and an active member's office are unchanged. `noDeskNotice` takes `hasDesks`.

## Unreleased - A phone arrives at its desk (`sub/phone-landing`; not shipped)
- On a phone (under 600 px), arriving (the first fit of a page load) with more than one desk lands in desk mode on the desk it remembers, else the first, and wakes it as a one-desk member's arrival does. It used to land on the overview: a few small grey rectangles on a screen that fits one desk well. A saved overview or free camera doesn't change that; the home mark and Esc still go to the overview, and a reload after them lands on the desk again. Larger screens still arrive on the overview. A resize or a list change after arriving still never wakes a desk or brings back a member who zoomed out.
- Before the desk list answers, a phone paints the remembered desk instead of the overview (paint only: no view change, no wake), so there's no overview flash and then a jump. `web/src/shell/actions.ts` (`refit`). The phone carousel and `carouselOn` rules are untouched.
- On a phone in desk mode, the neighbouring desk that peeked in at the screen edge isn't drawn (a clipped desk with a count badge read as a glitch; "2 of 3" says there are more). The overview and larger screens draw every desk. `canvas/WorldStage.tsx` wraps each desk in a Konva group that is invisible for `awayOnPhone` (`canvas/awayOnPhone.ts`); a hidden group draws and takes no touch. Arriving on a phone doesn't raise the desk, so it never rewrites the saved stacking.
- Tests: `shell/arrival.test.ts` (7), `canvas/awayOnPhone.test.ts` (2), `phone.landing.shell.spec.ts` (8, at 390 x 844, incl. a pixel check of the edge columns that fails with the hiding off); `desks.shell.spec.ts`'s phone drag now starts from the overview.

## Unreleased - The desk app contract's base in deskd (launch row S-97, task 18; `sub/app-contract-deskd`; not released)
- **Added:** `deskd`'s half of the desk app contract (`docs/decisions/draft-desk-app-contract.md`, its Desk Engineer review of Nov 11; `services/deskd/docs/API.md` §19). It installs signed packages under `/data/apps`, checking the catalog's signature and `seq`, both of the package's signatures, the manifest, every file's hash and the layout before anything runs, and ends with a trial start that rolls back on failure. It upgrades and removes (the data stays unless asked), serves the catalog to the Desk app, and relays `apps.call`, `apps.mutate` and the `app:<id>:<topic>` topics to each app's own socket, telling the app who calls only by pseudonyms. On a Guild Desk only the Founder manages apps. On, with a kill switch (`/data/desk/apps-switch`: `off`, or `install-off`). Inert on every desk today: no image has the System Engineer's helper, `/data/apps` or a review key, so nothing installs.
- **Changed:** `deskd`'s `run` mounts `/data/apps`, `/run/ma-apps` and the review keys' directories, read-only for the keys, only where the image has made them.
- Tests: 65 in `services/deskd/src/test/java/…/apps/`, and `RealTallyIT` against the sample's Go app; `test-release-scripts.sh` checks the new mounts.

## Unreleased - deskd tells the Desk app when a page's protected video can't play (TD-225; `sub/drm-detect`; in no release)
- **Added (`deskd`):** a tab whose page asked for Widevine, PlayReady or FairPlay and was refused, as the desk's Chromium always refuses, gets `protectedMedia: {at, keySystem}` in `browser.tab.list` and on `browser.tabs`, once per page, cleared by a navigation (`services/deskd/docs/API.md` §10, "A protected video that can't play"). A fixed script in the page's top frame wraps `navigator.requestMediaKeySystemAccess` and reports through deskd's isolated world, with `Runtime.enable` never sent; the page gets Chromium's own answer. No site is blocked. The note itself is the Desk app's, not built yet.

## Unreleased - The Agent asks before it acts (S-14; `sub/agent-approvals`; desk-agent and deskd, needs their next release; the cards need the Agent app)
- **Added:** every agent run's tool calls go through an approval gate. A write, delete, send, spend or egress call (and a memory save after reading untrusted content) waits for the member's yes on a card bound to the SHA-256 of its exact payload; a decline, an hour without an answer (a day for a run a timer or another agent started), a stop or a restart does nothing. `agent.approvals.list`, `agent.approval.get` and `agent.approval.decide`, the `approval` event, and a waiting reply's `awaiting_approval` (deskd `API.md` §18). Spending is never asked from an unattended run, the same declined call isn't asked again, and asking stops after 3 declines in a run. Each run's untrusted and private flags follow its reply, the next run and the thread summary; every ask, decision and call is in the audit log by ids only.
- **Added (deskd):** the relay of the three methods; a decide from an owner token without input is refused `permission` (a guild member decides with their own token, for their own agent); each yes leaves deskd's own single-use ticket, bound to the hash, for 10 minutes, which the tools deskd runs for desk-agent must redeem (`approval_unknown`, `approval_used`, `approval_mismatch`); an agent waiting for the member no longer counts as busy in the heartbeat.

## Unreleased - Phone leftovers from the free member walk and the clip re-shoot (`sub/desk-walk-leftovers`; not shipped)
- **Fixed:** the Browser's "Real page here from now on · Undo" line no longer leaves a lone "·" before Undo on a phone (`web/src/simple/BarSwitch.tsx`). The dot is part of the sentence now, so it hides with it on a phone held upright and is cut first by the ellipsis on a phone on its side. Under 700 px on its side the sentence goes for screen readers as it does upright (it was a sliver at 641 px and "Re…" at 667), so the icon and Undo show. A mouse sees no change.
- **Fixed:** on a phone the Agent window's head no longer cuts the agent's name ("Man…" for Manager at 360 px). In a column of 440 px or less the name and its line keep the head's first row and the five detail buttons go to a second row at its end, as the Browser's switch does on a phone held upright; the drawer opens under both rows (`web/agent/src/ui/agent.css`). Wider windows are unchanged.
- Tests: `touch.shell.spec.ts` checks that no "·" shows without its sentence on every phone and tablet, and at 641 and 667 px on its side, in all three themes; `agent.shell.spec.ts` checks that "Manager" fits a 360 px phone's head and that an open panel covers none of the detail buttons, in all three themes. Each fails on the code before.

## Unreleased - Agents remember, and a long thread no longer breaks (S-14; `sub/agent-launch-left`; desk-agent and deskd, needs their next release; the memory screen needs the Agent app)
- **Added:** each agent remembers short lines about its member across conversations (`memory_save`, `memory_forget`), pinned into every later run as data; the member sees and deletes them, one or all, with `agent.memory.list`, `.delete` and `.clear` (deskd relays them, a guild member's for their own agent). A save in a run that read untrusted content needs the member's yes and keeps the mark; loaded memory marks the run as holding private data (Security's rules on the tools and approvals protocol).
- **Fixed:** a thread past the model's context failed every send for good, and one whose estimate passed the run's $2 was refused for good. A run now sends the thread's summary and its newest messages within 48,000 estimated tokens, and a compaction after the run folds the rest into a summary the member can see and delete.

## Unreleased - the Browser's address box is empty on the desk's start page (`sub/browser-home-address-2`)
- **Fixed:** a fresh desk's Browser showed `file:///usr/local/share/ma/home.html` in the address box; deskd now reports no address for it, and `browser/address.ts` keeps a narrow guard for the same path (also with `?` or `#`) so a desk on an older deskd shows the empty box and its placeholder too. Any other page, `file://` included, shows as before.

## Unreleased - Notebook's activity comes a page at a time (`sub/notebook-launch-left`, S-13; deskd and desk-notebook, not released)
- **Fixed:** `notebook.activity` no longer answers `too_large` past about 7,000 rows (a few years of notes). It takes `cursor` and answers `{rows, next}`: as many rows as fit one reply, newest day first, and `next` for the rest, as `notebook.tree` does. A client that sends no cursor gets everything it got before. deskd relays it unchanged; on a guild desk every member still sees only live pages' rows. API.md section 13, `services/notebook/README.md`. Tests: desk-notebook `activity.test.ts` (40,000 rows, a guild desk), deskd `NotebookRelayTest` and `RealDeskNotebookActivityIT` (30,000 rows through the relay to the real service).

## Unreleased - A free member's Files footer reads against the 2 GB cap (`sub/files-free-line`; needs the next Desk app release, deskd unchanged)
- **Added:** on a free desk (`files.usage` carries `capBytes` and `capUsedBytes`, ADR 0090) the Files footer reads "1.4 GB of 2 GB used" against the cap (the tree, the trash and unfinished uploads), its bar is the cap, and at 90% and when full a calm second line follows ("{alliance} has a {allianceDrive} drive." / "Your drive is full. Delete something to make room, or get {allianceDrive} with {alliance}.") with See membership, from the free office's `useFree` `drive` hook. A paid or older desk keeps today's line. The code is in Files' lazy chunk (initial JS 243.5 to 243.7 KB of 250). Tests: `words.test.ts`, `files.shell.spec.ts` (free: ok, near, full in three themes; paid).

## Unreleased - 4 KB off the Desk app's first load, nothing a member sees changed (`sub/desk-bundle-headroom`, reviewed and brought level with Zen as `sub/desk-bundle-headroom-2`; not shipped)
- **Changed (no visible change):** `npm run build:release` goes from 247.4 KB (main, `f4967496`) to 243.5 KB of its 250 KB budget, both built the same way, and the stylesheet is byte for byte main's (built with the same `MA_VERSION`). The first load is still two levels deep (the entry, then what it imports). What moved never draws on the first paint and is fetched once the page is idle, beside the Browser's code, so from then on it opens in the same commit as before.
- **The window's frame** (`windows/AppWindow.tsx`: its frame, drag and resize): no window outlives a page load (`state/windowStore.ts`), so `WindowLayer` renders it from a chunk through `lib/onDemand.ts` (no `React.lazy` or Suspense, so no fallback or throttled reveal). **Every app's window stays in the first load** (`apps/registry.tsx` and what it imports): a desk in carousel mode, or a phone's carousel restored from this tab, renders its app from it as the office comes up, and the review found that out of the first load the stage waited for two more chunks, one after the other (4.0 KB in one round trip on main, 12.3 KB in three without it). The Notebook editor's loader and prefetch are `notebook/prefetch.ts`.
- **The account menu's, the bell's and the bandwidth circle's panels** (`chrome/AccountPanel.tsx`, `chrome/BellPanel.tsx`, `bandwidth/BandwidthPanel.tsx`, one chunk through `chrome/popoversChunk.ts`): the buttons, the ring, the count and the bell's list fetch stay in the first load; only what the open panel draws moved. The menus' state (sign-out progress, plain background) still lives in the button's component. Zen's account menu came over whole: the Background picker (its own chunk, fetched when the menu opens, as on main), the theme names from `THEME_LABELS` ("Use Zen when the system is dark") and the plain background's hint.
- **An agent's uploaded mark** (`agent/roster.ts`): the PNG helpers load beside the icon's own fetch, not with the office.
- **`main.tsx` imports `theme/tokens`** beside `themeStore`: with the account menu's panel a chunk, nothing in the entry imported it any more, and it became a third level of the first load, one more round trip before the first paint.
- **A window or panel opened before its chunk is here** shows as the chunk lands (a window still grows from its tile and takes the keyboard). After the idle fetch, and from cache, it's on screen before the next frame after the press, as on main.
- Tests: Vitest `lib/onDemand.test.ts` (new); Playwright `onDemand.shell.spec.ts` (new: a window and the account panel at the next frame after the press once fetched; a window and the panel pressed before their chunk lands; the account menu in Light, Zen and Dark, whose shots are byte for byte main's). Counts are in `docs/sub-status/desk-bundle-headroom.md`.
